Then like the noise of some city heard amongst birds in woods, like a sob heard amongst children that are all met to rejoice, like laughter amongst a company that weep, like a shrill wind in orchards amongst the early blossom, like a wolf coming over the downs where the sheep are asleep, there came a feeling into the Elf King's mood that one was coming towards them across the fields of Earth. It was Alveric with his sword of thunderbolt-iron, which somehow the old King sensed by its flavour of magic.

===Chinese===
随后，如同林间鸟鸣之中传来的一座城市的喧嚣，如同一群欢聚的孩童中传来的啜泣，如同一群悲泣者中传来的笑声，如同果园初开的花间刮起的一阵凛冽寒风，如同羊群沉睡的丘陵上走来的一匹狼——一种感觉涌上了精灵国王的心头：有一个人正穿过人世的原野向他们走来。那是阿尔韦里克，手持他那柄雷石之铁铸成的剑——老国王是借着它身上那一缕魔法的气息而觉察到它的。

===Original===
Then the Elf King rose, and put his left arm about his daughter, and raised his right to make a mighty enchantment, standing up before his shining throne which is the very centre of Elfland. And with clear resonance deep down in his throat he chaunted a rhythmic spell, all made of words that Lirazel never had heard before, some age-old incantation, calling Elfland away, drawing it further from Earth. And the marvellous flowers heard as their petals drank in the music, and the deep notes flooded the lawns; and all the palace thrilled, and quivered with brighter colours; and a charm went over the plain as far as the frontier of twilight, and a trembling went through the enchanted wood. Still the Elf King chaunted on. The ringing ominous notes came now to the Elfin Mountains, and all their line of peaks quivered as hills in haze, when the heat of summer beats up from the moors and visibly dances in air. All Elfland heard, all Elfland obeyed that spell. And now the King and his daughter drifted away, as the smoke of the nomads drifts over Sahara away from their camel's-hair tents, as dreams drift away at dawn, as clouds over the sunset; and like the wind with the smoke, night with the dreams, warmth with the sunset, all Elfland drifted with them. All Elfland drifted with them and left the desolate plain, the dreary deserted region, the unenchanted land. So swiftly that spell was uttered, so suddenly Elfland obeyed, that many a little song, old memory, garden or May tree of remembered years, was swept but a little way by the drift and heave of Elfland, swaying too slowly eastwards till the elfin lawns were gone, and the barrier of twilight heaved over them and left them among the rocks.

===Chinese===
于是精灵国王起身，左臂环住他的女儿，举起右手，立在他那光辉的宝座前——那宝座便是精灵国正正的中心——要作一道伟大的魔法。他以喉间深沉而清晰的共鸣，吟唱起一段有节奏的咒语，全由莉拉泽尔从未听过的字句构成，某种古老的咒文，呼唤精灵国离去，将它从人世那里拉得更远。那些神奇的花朵倾听着，花瓣如饮音乐一般吸纳那旋律，深沉的音调漫过了草坪；整座宫殿为之颤动，因更明亮的色彩而摇曳；一道魔力掠过平原，一直延伸到暮色的边境，一阵颤栗穿过了那片施过魔法的森林。精灵国王仍在吟唱。那鸣响而不祥的音调此刻传到了精灵山脉，那整列山峰如雾中的群山一般颤动起来——正如夏日酷热从荒原上蒸腾而起、明明可见地在空气中跳舞之时一般。整个精灵国都听见了，整个精灵国都服从了那道咒语。于是国王与他的女儿飘然而去，如同游牧者的炊烟飘过撒哈拉、远离他们的驼毛帐篷，如同梦在黎明时分飘散，如同云在日落之上飘移；如同风伴着烟、夜伴着梦、温暖伴着日落，整个精灵国也随他们一同飘移。整个精灵国随他们一同飘移，留下那荒凉的原野、那凄凉废弃的地域、那不再有魔法的土地。那咒语念得如此之快，精灵国服从得如此之突然，以至于许多小小的歌谣、旧日的记忆、故年之园圃或五月的花树，只被精灵国的漂移与涌动稍稍卷动了一程，向东摆动得太慢，终于当精灵的草坪已逝去时，暮色的屏障便涌过了它们，将它们留在了岩石之间。

===Original===
And whither Elfland went I cannot say, nor even whether it followed the curve of the Earth or drifted beyond our rocks out into twilight: there had been an enchantment near to our fields and now there was none: wherever it went it was far.

===Chinese===
至于精灵国去了何处，我说不出，甚至说不清它是循着大地的弧度而去，还是飘过了我们的岩石、飘入了暮色之中：原本有一片魔法邻近我们的原野，如今已没有了：无论它去了哪里，它都已遥远。

===Original===
Then the Elf King ceased to chaunt and all was accomplished. As silently as, in a moment that none can determine, the long layers over the sunset turn from gold to pink, or from a glowing pink to a listless unlit colour, all Elfland left the edges of those fields by which its wonder had lurked for long ages of men, and was away now whither I know not. And the Elf King seated himself again on his throne of mist and ice, in which charmed rainbows were, and took Lirazel his daughter again on his knee, and the calm that his chaunting had broken came back heavy and deep over Elfland. Heavy and deep it fell on the lawns, heavy and deep on the flowers; each dazzling blade of grass was still in its little curve as though Nature in a moment of mourning said "Hush" at the sudden end of the world; and the flowers dreamed on in their beauty, immune from autumn or wind. Far over the moors of the trolls slept the calm of the King of Elfland, where the smoke from their queer habitations hung stilled in the air; and in a forest wherein it quieted the trembling of myriads of petals on roses, it stilled the pools where the great lilies towered, till they and their reflections slept on in one gorgeous dream. And there below motionless fronds of dream-gripped trees, on the still water dreaming of the still air, where the huge lily-leaves floated green in the calm, was the troll Lurulu, sitting upon a leaf. For thus they named in Elfland the troll that had gone to Erl. He sat there gazing into the water at a certain impudent look that he had on. He gazed and gazed and gazed.

===Chinese===
于是精灵国王停止了吟唱，一切便已成就。正如没有人能判定落日上方层层云霞在哪个瞬间由金转为粉、又由灼灼的粉转作一种慵懒的黯淡之色——一切都悄无声息地发生——整个精灵国离开了那些原野的边缘，在那无数人世的漫长岁月里，它的神奇曾潜伏于那些边缘之旁，而如今它已远去，去了何处我无从知晓。精灵国王又在他那由薄雾与冰构成、内含着魔法彩虹的宝座上坐了下来，又将他的女儿莉拉泽尔抱在膝上，而那因他吟唱而被打破的宁静，又深沉浓重地回到了精灵国之上。它沉沉地、深深地落在草坪上，沉沉地、深深地落在花朵上；每一片耀眼的草叶都静定在它那小小的弧度里，仿佛大自然在哀悼的一刻对着这骤然终结的世界轻声说了句「嘘」；而花朵们继续在它们的美中做梦，不染秋霜，不侵风雨。在巨怪们的荒原之外很远的地方，精灵国王的宁静沉沉睡去，他们那些古怪居所升起的炊烟也静悬于空中；在一座森林中，那宁静平息了千万片玫瑰花瓣的颤动，它也平息了巨大的百合花挺立其上的那些池水，直到那些花与它们的倒影一同沉入一个绚烂的梦中继续酣眠。而在那被梦所攫住的树木的无声蕨叶之下，在那梦见着静止空气的静水之上，在巨大的百合叶于宁静中碧绿地漂浮之处，是巨怪卢鲁鲁，坐在一片叶子上面。因为在精灵国里，他们便是这样称呼那个曾去厄尔的巨怪的。他坐在那里，凝望着水中自己脸上那副放肆的神情。他望啊、望啊、望啊。

===Original===
Nothing stirred, nothing changed. All things were still, reposing in the deep content of the King. The Knight of the Guard brought his sword back to the carry, and afterwards stood as still at his perpetual post as some suit of armour whose owner is centuries dead. And still the King sat silent with his daughter upon his knee, his blue eyes unmoving as the pale-blue peaks, which through wide windows shone from the Elfin Mountains.

===Chinese===
什么也不动，什么也不变。万物都静默着，安憩于国王那深深的满足之中。卫队的骑士将剑收回持剑的姿态，然后便如一具甲胄——它的主人已死去数百年——那般静止地守在他永恒的岗位上。而国王仍静默地坐着，女儿在膝上，他蓝色的双眼如淡蓝色的山峰一般纹丝不动，那山峰透过宽阔的窗户从精灵山脉那边闪耀过来。

===Original===
And the Elf King stirred not, nor changed; but held to that moment in which he had found content; and laid its influence over all his dominions, for the good and welfare of Elfland; for he had what all our troubled world with all its changes seeks, and finds so rarely and must at once cast it away. He had found content and held it.

===Chinese===
精灵国王不动，也不变；只是守住了他寻得满足的那个瞬间；并将它的影响铺展到他全部的领地之上，为精灵国的福祉与安宁；因为他所拥有的，正是我们这动荡不安、变幻不定的整个世界所寻觅、却极少寻得、即便寻得也必立刻抛却的东西。他寻得了满足，并将它守住。

===Original===
And in that calm that settled down upon Elfland there passed ten years over the fields we know.

===Chinese===
在那降临精灵国的宁静之中，在我们所知的原野之上，十年便这样过去了。

## XXVII The Return of Lurulu / 二十七 卢鲁鲁之归

===Original===
Over hamlet and Castle of Erl, and through every nook and crevice of it, spring passed; a mild benediction that blessed the very air and sought out all living things; not missing even the tiny plants that had their dwelling in most secluded places, under eaves, in the cracks of old barrels, or along the lines of mortar that held ancient rows of stones. And in this season Orion hunted no unicorns; not that he knew in what season the unicorns bred in Elfland, where time is not as here; but because of a feeling he had from all his earthly forefathers against hunting any creature in this season of song and flowers. So he tended his hounds and often watched the hills, expecting on any day the return of Lurulu.

===Chinese===
春光掠过厄尔的村落与城堡，穿过它的每一个角落与缝隙；这是一次温和的祝福，祝福了那空气本身，寻遍了一切生灵；便是在最幽僻之处栖身的微小草木——屋檐之下、旧桶的裂痕里，或沿着黏合古旧石列的灰泥缝中——也不曾遗漏。在这个时节奥利安不去猎独角兽；倒不是他知道独角兽在精灵国里于何季繁殖——那里的时间与这里不同——而是出于一种得自他一切人间先祖的感觉，使他不愿在这歌声与繁花之季猎取任何生灵。于是他照料他的群犬，又时常眺望群山，盼着卢鲁鲁随时归来。

===Original===
And spring passed by and the summer flowers grew, and still there was no sign of the troll returning, for time moves through the dells of Elfland as over no field of man. And long Orion watched through fading evenings till the line of the hills was black, yet never saw the small round heads of trolls bobbing across the downs.

===Chinese===
春光逝去，夏花生长，那巨怪归来仍无半点征兆，因为时间流过精灵国的谷地，与流过任何一片人间田地都不相同。奥利安久久地在渐渐暗淡的暮色中守望，直到山脊的轮廓化作一片漆黑，却始终不曾看见巨怪那又小又圆的脑袋在丘陵上颠簸掠过。

===Original===
And the long autumnal winds came sighing out of cold lands, and found Orion still watching for Lurulu; and the mist and the turning leaves spoke to his heart of hunting. And the hounds were whining for the open spaces and the line of scent like a mysterious path crossing the wide world, but Orion would hunt nothing less than unicorns, and waited yet for his trolls.

===Chinese===
漫长的秋风自寒土长叹而至，发觉奥利安仍在守望卢鲁鲁；薄雾与变色的落叶向他的心诉说着狩猎。群犬呜咽着渴望那开阔之地，渴望那一线气息犹如一条神秘的路径横贯辽阔的世界，然而奥利安除了独角兽之外什么也不肯猎，于是仍等着他的巨怪。

===Original===
And one of these earthly days, with a menace of frost in the air and a scarlet sunset, Lurulu's talk to the trolls in the wood being finished, and their scamper swifter than hares having brought them soon to the frontier, those in our fields who looked (as they seldom did) towards that mysterious border where Earth ended might have seen the unwonted shapes of the nimble trolls coming all grey through the evening. They came dropping, troll after troll, from the soaring leaps they took high through the boundary of twilight; and, landing thus unceremoniously in our fields, came capering, somersaulting and running, with gusts of impudent laughter, as though this were a proper manner in which to approach by no means the least of the planets.

===Chinese===
在人间的这样一个日子，空气里带着降霜的征兆，落日一片猩红，卢鲁鲁在林中对众巨怪的那番话已讲完，它们比野兔还快的蹦跳很快将它们带到边境，于是在我们原野中那些朝那道人间终止之处的神秘边境张望的人（他们难得如此张望），或许看见了那些身手敏捷的巨怪不同寻常的身影，在暮色中通体灰色地走来。它们一只接一只地从高跃穿过暮色屏障的腾跳中落下地来；就这样毫不客气地落在我们原野中之后，便蹦跳着、翻着筋斗、奔跑着，迸发出一阵阵放肆的大笑，仿佛这便是去接近一颗绝非微不足道的行星的得体方式。

===Original===
They rustled by the small houses like the wind passing through straw, and none that heard the light rushing sound of their passing knew how outlandish they were, except the dogs, whose work it is to watch, and who know of all things that pass, their degree of remoteness to man. At gipsies, tramps, and all that go without houses, dogs bark whenever they pass; at the wild things of the woods they bark with greater abhorrence, knowing well the rebellious contempt in which they hold man; at the fox, for his touch of mystery and his far wanderings, they bark more furiously: but tonight the barking of dogs was beyond all abhorrence and fury; many a farmer this night believed that his dog was choking.

===Chinese===
它们从那些小屋旁窸窣而过，如同风穿过稻草，听见它们经过那轻巧窸窣之声的人，没有谁知道它们是何等怪异之物，只有狗除外——狗的职责便是守望，凡一切经过之物，它们都晓得它们与人相距之远近。吉卜赛人、流浪汉，以及一切无家可归之人经过时，狗便朝他们吠；林中的野物经过时，它们吠得更加厌恶，深谙那些野物对人怀着何等叛逆的轻蔑；对狐狸，因它带着一丝神秘、又走得很远，它们吠得更为狂怒：可今夜狗的狂吠已超出了一切厌恶与狂怒；今夜许多农人都以为自己的狗要噎死了。

===Original===
And passing over these fields, staying not to laugh at the clumsy scared running of sheep, for they kept their laughter for man, they came soon to the downs above Erl; and there below them was night and the smoke of men, all grey together. And not knowing from what slight causes the smoke arose, here from a woman boiling a kettle of water, or there because one dried the frock of a child, or that a few old men might warm their hands in the evening, the trolls forbore to laugh as they had planned to do as soon as they should meet with the things of man. Perhaps even they, whose gravest thoughts were just under the surface of laughter, even they were a little awed by the strangeness and nearness of man sleeping there in his hamlet with all his smoke about him. Though awe in these light minds rested no longer than does the squirrel on the thin extremest twigs.

===Chinese===
它们越过这些田野，并不停下来笑那绵羊笨拙惊恐的奔逃——因为它们把笑声留给了人——很快便来到了厄尔上方的丘陵；在它们下方便是夜色与人间的炊烟，一并灰蒙蒙的。它们不知道那些炊烟因着多么微不足道的缘由升起：这里是一个女人在烧一壶水，那里是有人在烘一个孩子的罩衣，又或是有几个老人要在暮色里暖暖手——巨怪们便忍住了笑，不再如它们原先所打算的那样，一旦遇见人间之物便要大笑。也许连它们——它们最郑重的念头也不过藏在笑声的表层之下——也许连它们也略略为人的奇异与切近而生出几分敬畏，人就这样睡在他的村落里，周身缭绕着他的炊烟。不过敬畏在这些轻飘的心思里停留，并不比松鼠栖在又细又最高的枝梢上更久。

===Original===
In a while they lifted their eyes up from the valley, and there was the western sky still shining above the last of the gloaming, a little strip of colour and dying light, so lovely that they believed that another elfland lay the other side of the valley, two dim diaphonous magical elfin lands hemming in this valley and few fields of men close upon either side. And, sitting there on the hillside peering westward, the next thing they saw was a star: it was Venus low in the west brimming with blueness. And they all bowed their heads many times to this pale-blue beautiful stranger; for though politeness was rare with them they saw that the Evening Star was nothing of Earth and no affair of man's, and believed it came out of that elfland they did not know on the western side of the world. And more and more stars appeared, till the trolls were frightened, for they knew nothing of these glittering wanderers that could steal out of the darkness and shine: at first they said "There are more trolls than stars," and were comforted, for they trusted greatly in numbers. Then there were soon more stars than trolls; and the trolls were ill at ease as they sat in the dark underneath all that multitude. But presently they forgot the fancy that troubled them, for no thought remained with them long. They turned their light attention instead to the yellow lights that glowed here and there on the hither side of the greyness, where a few of the houses of men stood warm and snug near the trolls. A beetle went by, and they hushed their chatter to hear what he would say; but he droned by, going home, and they did not know his language. A dog far off was ceaselessly crying out, and filling all the still night with a note of warning. And the trolls were angry at the sound of his voice, for they felt that he interfered between them and man. Then a soft whiteness came out of the night and lit on the branch of a tree, and bowed its head to the left and looked at the trolls, and then bowed over to the right and looked at them again from there, and then back to the left again for it was not yet sure about them. "An owl," said Lurulu; and many besides Lurulu had seen his kind before, for he flies much along the edge of Elfland. Soon he was gone and they heard him hunting across the hills and the hollows; and then no sound was left but the voices of men, or the shrill shouts of children, and the bay of the dog that warned men against the trolls. "A sensible fellow," they said of the owl, for they liked the sound of his voice; but the voices of men and their dog sounded confused and tiresome.

===Chinese===
过了一会儿，它们从山谷上抬起眼来，西边的天空仍在那最后一缕暮霭之上泛着光，一窄条色彩与将熄之光，美得它们以为山谷的那一边还有另一个精灵国，两片朦胧、轻薄、神奇的精灵之地夹着这片山谷与这少许紧贴两侧的人间田野。坐在山坡上朝西张望，它们接下来看见的便是一颗星：那是金星，低低悬于西方，盈盈一派蓝色。它们齐齐向这位浅蓝的美丽异客一次次低下头去；因为虽然礼貌在它们之间颇为罕见，它们却看出这颗昏星绝非人间之物、也绝非人之事，并相信它来自它们所不知的那片在世界西方的精灵国。越来越多的星出现了，直到巨怪们害怕起来，因为它们对这些能从黑暗中潜出并闪闪发光的璀璨浪游者一无所知：起初它们说「巨怪比星星还多」，便心安了，因为它们极信赖数目。不久星星便多过了巨怪；巨怪们坐在黑暗中，在那繁多之下颇不自在。但随即它们便忘了那困扰它们的念头，因为没有任何念头能在它们心中久留。它们把那轻飘的注意力转向了灰色这一侧这里那里亮着的黄光——人间的几所房屋就在巨怪附近，温暖而舒适。一只甲虫路过，它们便噤了喋喋之声，想听听他会说些什么；可他嗡嗡地飞过，回家去了，它们听不懂他的话。远处一只狗不停地叫着，用一声声警号填满了整个寂静的夜。巨怪们听见它的声音便恼怒起来，因为它们觉得它碍在了它们与人之间。随即一团柔和的白色从夜色中出来，落在一根树枝上，把头偏向左边看了看巨怪，又偏向右边从那边再看它们一眼，又再回到左边，因为它对它们还没拿准。「一只猫头鹰，」卢鲁鲁说；卢鲁鲁以外还有许多位以前见过它的同类，因为它常沿精灵国的边境飞。不久它便走了，它们听见它越过山丘与谷地一路猎食；此后再无别的声音，只除了人的声音、孩童尖利的叫喊，以及那条警告人提防巨怪的狗的吠声。「一个明白事理的家伙，」它们这样评价那猫头鹰，因为它们喜欢它的嗓音；而人与他们的狗的声音却听起来杂乱而恼人。

===Original===
They saw sometimes the lights of late wayfarers crossing the downs towards Erl, or heard men that cheered themselves in the lonely night by singing, instead of by lantern's light. And all the while the Evening Star grew bigger, and great trees grew blacker and blacker.

===Chinese===
它们有时看见迟归的旅人提着灯火越过丘陵往厄尔去，又或听见有人在孤寂的夜里以唱歌来壮胆，而非借灯笼的光。而那昏星一直在变大，大树则越来越黑。

===Original===
Then from underneath the smoke and the mist of the stream there boomed all of a sudden the brazen bell of the Freer out of deep night in the valley. Night and the slopes of Erl and the dark downs echoed with it; and the echoes rode up to the trolls and seemed to challenge them, with all accursed things and wandering spirits and bodies unblessed of the Freer.

===Chinese===
随即，从炊烟与小溪的雾气之下，山谷深夜里忽地轰然响起教士那铜钟。夜与厄尔的坡地与漆黑的丘陵都为之共鸣；那回响升上巨怪们所在之处，仿佛带着一切受诅咒之物、浪游之灵与未受教士祝福的躯体，向它们叫阵。

===Original===
And the solemn sound of those echoes going alone through the night from every heavy swing of the holy bell cheered that band of trolls among all the strangeness of Earth, for whatever is solemn always moves trolls to levity. They turned merrier now and tittered among themselves.

===Chinese===
那回响在夜中独自穿行的庄严之声——每一记沉重钟摆而来——竟使这一队巨怪在人间诸般的陌生之中振奋起来，因为凡属庄严之物，总使巨怪生轻浮之念。它们如今更为快活，彼此窃窃嗤笑。

===Original===
And while they still watched all that host of stars, wondering if they were friendly, the sky grew steely blue and the eastern stars dwindled, and the mist and the smoke of men turned white, and a radiance touched the further edge of the valley; and the moon came up over the downs behind the trolls. Then voices sang from the holy place of the Freer, chaunting moon matins; which it was their wont to sing on nights of the full moon while the moon was yet low. And this rite they named moon's-morning. The bell had ceased, chance voices spoke no more, they had hushed their dog in the valley and silenced his warning, and lonely and grave and solemn that people's song floated up from before the candles in their small square sacred place, built of grey stone by men that were dead for ages and ages; all solemn the song welled up in the time of the moon's rising, grave as the night, mysterious as the full moon, and fraught with a meaning that was far beyond the highest thoughts of the trolls. Then the trolls leaped up with one accord from the frosted grass of the downs and all poured down the valley to laugh at the ways of men, to mock at their sacred things and to dare their singing with levity.

===Chinese===
当它们仍在守望那满天星斗、揣度它们是否友善之际，天空变成了铁青的蓝色，东方的星渐次稀微，雾气与人间的炊烟转白，一道光辉触到了山谷的那一边缘；月亮从巨怪身后的丘陵上升起。随即，歌声从教士的圣所传来，诵唱月之晨课；那是他们在满月之夜、月亮尚低之时惯常所唱的。他们将此仪式称作「月之清晨」。钟声已歇，偶然的人声不再，他们已在山谷中噤住了他们的狗、止住了它的警告，于是那庄重的歌声从他们小小的方形圣所中、从那些烛火之前飘升起来——那圣所以灰石砌成，砌它的人已死去千代万代；歌声在月升之际庄严地涌起，肃穆如夜，神秘如满月，蕴着一种远超巨怪们最高念头的意涵。于是巨怪们齐齐从凝霜的丘陵草皮上一跃而起，全体涌下山谷，去嘲笑人间的诸般做派，讥嘲他们的圣物，并以轻浮去挑衅他们的歌声。

===Original===
Many a rabbit rose up and fled from their onrush, and thrills of laughter arose from the trolls at their fear. A meteor flashed westwards, racing after the sun; either as a portent to warn the hamlet of Erl that folk from beyond Earth's borders approached them now, or else in fulfilment of some natural law. To the trolls it seemed that one of the proud stars fell, and they rejoiced with elvish levity.

===Chinese===
许多兔子从它们冲来的势头中惊起奔逃，巨怪们因它们的惊恐而迸发出一阵阵大笑。一颗流星朝西一闪，追赶着太阳而去；这或者是为警告厄尔的村落：来自人间边境之外的族类此刻正向他们逼近；又或者是某条自然律的应验。在巨怪看来，却仿佛是一颗高傲的星落了下来，它们便以精灵的轻浮为此欢腾。

===Original===
Thus they came giggling through the night, and ran down the street of the village, unseen as any wild creature that roams late through the darkness; and Lurulu led them to the pigeon-loft, and they all poured clambering in. Some rumour arose in the village that a fox had jumped into the pigeon-loft, but it ceased almost as soon as the pigeons returned to their homes, and the folk of Erl had no more hint till the morning that something had entered their village from beyond the borders of Earth.

===Chinese===
它们就这样嗤笑着穿过黑夜，跑下村里的那条街，如同任何深夜在黑暗中游荡的野兽一般无人察觉；卢鲁鲁将它们领到鸽笼前，它们便一涌而上攀了进去。村里曾一度有谣言说一只狐狸跳进了鸽笼，但这谣言几乎在鸽子们归巢之时便平息了，厄尔之民直到早晨之前再无丝毫察觉：有什么东西已从人间边境之外进入了他们的村庄。

===Original===
In a brown mass thicker than young pigs are along the edge of a trough the trolls encumbered the floor of the pigeons' home. And time went over them as over all earthly things. And well they knew, though tiny was their intelligence, that by crossing the border of twilight they incurred the wasting of time; for nothing dwells by the brink of any danger and lives ignorant of its menace: as conies in rocky altitudes know the peril of the sheer cliff, so they that dwell near Earth's border knew well the danger of time. And yet they came. The wonder and lure of Earth had been overstrong for them. Does not many a young man squander youth as they squandered immortality?

===Chinese===
巨怪们像一坨褐色的一团、比沿食槽挤作一排的小猪崽还要厚实，塞满了鸽子家园的地板。时间从它们身上越过，一如从一切人间之物身上越过。它们心里明白——纵然它们的智慧细小——只要越过暮色的边境，便会招致时光的消磨；因为没有任何生灵栖于某种危险的边缘却对其威胁懵然不知地活着：正如栖于高岩的兔儿知道悬崖绝壁之险，这些住在人间边境附近的，也深知时间之危。然而它们还是来了。人间的奇景与诱惑对它们而言太过强大。岂不见多少少年人挥霍青春，正如同它们挥霍了那不朽之身？

===Original===
And Lurulu showed them how to hold off time for a while, which otherwise would make them older and older each moment and whirl them on with Earth's restlessness all night long. Then he curled up his knees and shut his eyes and lay still. This, he told them, was sleep; and, cautioning them to continue to breathe, though being still in other respects, he then slept in earnest: and after some vain attempts the brown trolls did the same.

===Chinese===
卢鲁鲁便教它们如何暂时挡开时间——否则时间会使它们每一刻都越来越老，并带着人间的躁动整夜驱赶它们前行。然后他蜷起膝盖、闭上眼、一动不动地躺下。这，他告诉它们，就是睡觉；他叮嘱它们要继续呼吸，除此之外务必静止不动，随即当真睡了过去：那群褐色的巨怪试了几次徒劳之后，也照着做了。

===Original===
When sunrise came, awaking all earthly things, long rays came through the thirty little windows and awoke both birds and trolls. And the mass of trolls went to the windows to look at Earth, and the pigeons fluttered to rafters and jerked sidelong looks at the trolls. And there that heap of trolls would have stayed, crowded high on each other's shoulders, blocking the windows while they studied the variety and restlessness of Earth, finding them equal to the strangest fables that wayfarers had brought to them out of our fields; and, though Lurulu often reminded them, they had forgotten the haughty white unicorns that they were to hunt with dogs.

===Chinese===
日出时分，唤醒了一切人间之物，长长的光线穿过那三十扇小窗，唤醒了鸟与巨怪。那一大群巨怪涌到窗前去看人间，鸽子们则扑棱棱飞上屋椽，斜着眼朝巨怪们偷觑。那一堆巨怪本会一直待在那里，高高地挤在彼此的肩上，堵住窗户，研究着人间的花样与躁动——觉得它们与旅人从我们原野中带去的那些最为离奇的寓言不相上下；而尽管卢鲁鲁时常提醒它们，它们却已忘了那些要用犬去猎的高傲白色独角兽。

===Original===
But Lurulu after a while led them down from the loft and brought them to the kennels. And they climbed up the high palings and peered over the top at the hounds.

When the hounds saw those strange heads peering over the palings they made a great uproar. And presently folk came to see what troubled the hounds. And when they saw that mass of trolls all round the top of the palings they said to each other, and so said all that heard of it: "There is magic in Erl now."

===Chinese===
但卢鲁鲁过了一会儿便领着它们从阁楼下来，带它们去犬舍。它们爬上高高的栅栏，从顶上探出头去窥看那些猎犬。

猎犬们看见那些怪异的脑袋在栅栏顶上探看，便大声喧闹起来。不久人们便来看看是什么搅扰了猎犬。当他们看见那一大群巨怪围满了栅栏顶上，便彼此说道——凡听见这事的人也都这样说：「如今厄尔有魔法了。」

## VI The Rune of the Elf King / 六 精灵国王的符文

===Original===
On the high balcony of his gleaming tower the King of Elfland stood. Below him echoed yet the thousand steps. He had lifted his head to chant the rune that should hold his daughter in Elfland, and in that moment had seen her pass the murky barrier; which on this side, facing toward Elfland, is all lustrous with twilight, and on that side, facing towards the fields we know, is smoky and angry and dull. And now he had dropped his head till his beard lay mingled with his cape of ermine above his cerulean cloak, and stood there silently sorrowful, while time passed swift as ever over the fields we know.

===Chinese===
精灵国王立于他那辉耀之塔的高高的露台上。脚下那一千级阶梯犹自回响着回声。他原已昂起头来，欲诵那道本可将其女儿留住于精灵国的符文，却恰在那一刻看见她穿过了那道幽暗的屏障——这道屏障朝着精灵国的一面，通体闪烁着暮霭的光华，而朝着我们所知的原野的那一面，却如烟似雾、暴戾而沉闷。如今他已垂下了头，垂得那么低，以至他的胡须与他那貂皮斗篷纠缠在一处，盖在那袭天蓝色的大氅之上，就那样默然忧伤地立着，而时光则在我们所知的原野之上，一如既往地飞速流逝。

===Original===
And standing there all blue and white against his silver tower, aged by the passing of times of which we know nothing, before he imposed its eternal calm upon Elfland, he thought of his daughter amongst our pitiless years. For he knew, whose wisdom surpassed the confines of Elfland and touched our rugged fields, knew well the harshness of material things and all the turmoil of Time. Even as he stood there he knew that the years that assail beauty, and the myriad harshnesses that vex the spirit, were already about his daughter. And the days that remained to her now seemed scarce more to him, dwelling beyond the fret and ruin of Time, than to us might seem a briar rose’s hours when plucked and foolishly hawked in the streets of a city. He knew that there hung over her now the doom of all mortal things. He thought of her perishing soon, as mortal things must; to be buried amongst the rocks of a land that scorned Elfland and that held its most treasured myths to be of little account. And were he not the King of all that magical land, which held its eternal calm from his own mysterious serenity, he had wept to think of the grave in rocky Earth gripping that form that was so fair forever. Or else, he thought, she would pass to some paradise far from his knowledge, some heaven of which books told in the fields we know, for he had heard even of this. He pictured her on some apple-haunted hill, under blossoms of an everlasting April, through which flickered the pale gold halos of those that had cursed Elfland. He saw, though dimly for all his magical wisdom, the glory that only the blessed clearly see. He saw his daughter on those heavenly hills stretch out both arms, as he knew well she would, towards the pale-blue peaks of her elfin home, while never one of the blessed heeded her yearning. And then, though he was king of all that land, that had its everlasting calm from him, he wept and all Elfland shivered. It shivered as placid water shivers here if something suddenly touches it from our fields.

===Chinese===
他那一身蓝白立于他那银色的塔前，被那些我们所一无所知的岁月之流逝所苍老——在他将其永恒的宁静加诸精灵国之前——他心中想着他的女儿如今置身于我们那无情的年月之间。因为他的智慧原是超出了精灵国的疆界、触及到我们这粗粝的原野的，他深知物质之物的严酷，深知时光的一切扰攘。就在他立于那里的当儿，他便已知道：那些侵蚀美貌的年岁，那无数折磨心灵的严酷，已然缠上了他的女儿。在他看来——他置身于时光的焦虑与毁灭之外——她如今所余的时日，已不比在我们眼中一枝野蔷薇被折下、又被愚昧地在城市街头叫卖时所余的光阴多出多少。他知道她头上如今已悬起了凡一切必朽之物所有的劫数。他想她不久便会如一切必朽之物所必然那样死去；被埋在一片轻鄙精灵国、并将其最珍视的神话视若无物的岩石之地的乱石之间。若非他是那整片魔法之地的王——那片土地之永恒宁静正源自他自己那神秘的安谧——他一念及那岩石密布的人间之墓穴将攫住那一具原本永世秀美的身躯，便要落泪。否则，他想，她或将归于某个他所不知的天堂，某个我们所知的原野上的书本所讲述的天国，因为就连这个他也有所耳闻。他设想她置身于某座苹果树荫覆的山丘之上，在一个永恒四月的花影之下，其间闪烁着那些曾诅咒精灵国之人苍白金色的光轮。虽则以他一切通魔法的智慧所见仍属朦胧，他看见了那惟有蒙福之人方能清楚看见的荣耀。他看见他的女儿在那一切天国之山的山丘上伸出双臂——他深知她必会如此——朝向她那精灵故乡的淡蓝色群峰，而那些蒙福之人中无一理会她的渴望。于是，虽则是那整片土地之王，那片土地之永恒宁静正源自于他，他落下了泪，于是整片精灵国为之战栗。它如同此处一潭静水，若有什么突然从我们的原野触及它时那般战栗。

===Original===
Then the King turned and left his balcony and went in great haste down his brazen steps.

===Chinese===
于是精灵国王转过身去，离开了他的露台，匆匆忙忙地走下他那铜色的阶陛。

===Original===
He came clanging to the ivory doors that shut the tower below, and through them came to the throne-room of which only song may tell. And there he took a parchment out of a coffer and a plume from some fabulous wing, and dipping the plume into no earthly ink, wrote out a rune on the parchment. Then raising two fingers he made the minor enchantment whereby he summoned his guard. And no guard came.

===Chinese===
他铿铿锵锵地来到那封锁塔下层的象牙门前，穿过它们便进入了那个惟有歌谣方能述说的觐见之殿。在那里他从一只箱箧中取出一卷羊皮纸，又从某只传说中巨禽的翼上拔下一根羽翎，将那翎毛蘸入一种绝非人间所有的墨水，在羊皮纸上写下一道符文。然后他举起两指，作了那个用以召唤他的卫队的小小法术。然而没有一个卫兵前来。

===Original===
I have said that no time passed at all in Elfland. Yet the happening of events is in itself a manifestation of time, and no event can occur unless time pass. Now it is thus with time in Elfland: in the eternal beauty that dreams in that honied air nothing stirs or fades or dies, nothing seeks its happiness in movement or change or a new thing, but has its ecstasy in the perpetual contemplation of all the beauty that has ever been, and which always glows over those enchanted lawns as intense as when first created by incantation or song. Yet if the energies of the wizard’s mind arose to meet a new thing, then that power that had laid its calm upon Elfland and held back time troubled the calm awhile, and time for awhile shook Elfland. Cast anything into a deep pool from a land strange to it, where some great fish dreams, and green weeds dream, and heavy colours dream, and light sleeps; the great fish stirs, the colours shift and change, the green weeds tremble, the light wakes, a myriad things know slow movement and change; and soon the whole pool is still again. It was the same when Alveric passed through the border of twilight and right through the enchanted wood, and the King was troubled and moved, and all Elfland trembled.

===Chinese===
我曾说精灵国之中根本无所谓时光流逝。然而事件之发生本身即是时间的一种显现，除非时间流逝，否则无一事可以发生。精灵国中的时间原是如此：在那馥郁如蜜的空气里梦幻着的那种永恒的美之中，无一物移动、凋零或死去，无一物于运动、变化或新奇之物中寻求其幸福，却只在永恒凝望一切曾经存在之美中获得其狂喜——那种美永远辉耀于那些施过魔法的草坪之上，一如最初由咒语或歌谣所创造之时那般浓烈。然而若巫者之心的精力奋起以迎一新事，那么那曾将其宁静加诸精灵国、阻住时间之力便会搅扰那宁静片刻，时间便也片刻之间震动了精灵国。从一片与之全然陌生的土地上向一口深潭里投下任何一物——那深潭之中有大鱼在梦，有碧绿的水草在梦，有浓重的色彩在梦，有光在酣睡；大鱼便动了起来，色彩移动变幻，碧绿的水草颤抖，光醒了过来，无数事物缓缓地知觉了运动与变化；不久整潭之水又归于寂静。当阿尔韦里克（Alveric）穿过暮色的边境、径直穿过那片施过魔法的森林之时，情形便是如此，于是精灵国王为之烦扰、为之动摇，整片精灵国都战栗了。

===Original===
When the King saw that no guard came he looked into the wood which he knew to be troubled, through the deep mass of the trees, that were quivering yet with the coming of Alveric; he looked through the deeps of the wood and the silver walls of his palace, for he looked by enchantment, and there he saw the four knights of his guard lying stricken upon the ground with their thick elvish blood hanging out through slits in their armour. And he thought of the early magic whereby he had made the eldest, with a rune all newly inspired, before he had conquered Time. He passed out through the splendour and glow of one of his flashing portals, and over a gleaming lawn and came to the fallen guard, and saw the trees still troubled.

===Chinese===
国王见没有卫兵前来，便望向那片他已知被搅扰了的森林，透过那层层叠叠的、仍因阿尔韦里克的到来而颤抖的树丛深处；他透过森林的深处、透过他那宫殿的银色墙壁望去——因为他是凭借魔法而望——在那里他看见他那卫队的四名骑士倒卧于地，他们那稠厚的精灵之血从铠甲的裂缝中渗了出来。他想起早年那以一道全新感悟的符文造出最年长那位骑士的魔法——那时他尚未征服时光。他从一道辉光闪烁的门扉那辉煌与光辉之中走了出来，越过一片辉耀的草坪，来到那被击倒的卫队之前，看见那些树木仍隐隐不安。

===Original===
“There has been magic here,” said the King of Elfland.

===Chinese===
「此处曾有过魔法，」精灵国王说。

===Original===
And then though he only had three runes that could do such a thing, and though they only could be uttered once, and one was already written upon parchment to bring his daughter home, he uttered the second of his most magical runes over that elder knight that his magic had made long ago. And in the silence that followed the last words of the rune the rents in the moon-bright armour all clicked shut at once, and the thick dark blood was gone and the knight rose live to his feet. And the Elf King now had only one rune left that was mightier than any magic we know.

===Chinese===
于是，尽管他只有三道能行此事的符文，尽管它们每一道皆只能诵念一次，而其中一道已经写在了羊皮纸上用以将其女儿领回家中，他仍对着那由他的魔法很久以前所造的年长骑士，诵出了他那最具魔力的符文中的第二道。在符文末字之后的那片寂静里，那月辉般明亮的铠甲上的裂口全都「咔嗒」一声同时合拢了，那稠厚暗黑的血消失了，那骑士便活了过来，站起了身。至此精灵国王手中便只剩下一道符文，而那一道比我等所知的任何魔法都更为强大。

===Original===
The other three knights lay dead; and, having no souls, their magic returned again to the mind of their master.

===Chinese===
另外三名骑士倒卧已死；并且，由于他们并无灵魂，他们的魔法便复归于他们主人的心中。

===Original===
He went back then to his palace, while he sent the last of his guard to fetch him a troll.

Dark brown of skin and two or three feet high the trolls are a gnomish tribe that inhabit Elfland. And soon there was a scamper in the throne-room that may only be told of in song, and a troll lit by the throne on its two bare feet and stood before its king. The King gave it the parchment with the rune written thereon, saying: “Scamper hence, and pass over the end of the Land, until you come to the fields that none know here; and find the Princess Lirazel who is gone to the haunts of men, and give her this rune and she shall read it and all shall be well.”

===Chinese===
他于是回到他的宫殿，同时派遣他最后的一名卫兵去替他抓一只巨怪（troll）。

巨怪是居于精灵国的一支地精之族，皮肤深褐，身高不过两三尺。不久觐见之殿里便响起一阵窸窸窣窣的蹦跳之声——那情景惟有歌谣方能述说——一只巨怪以其两只赤裸的脚落在了宝座旁，立在其国王之前。国王将那写着符文的羊皮纸交给它，说道：「由此蹦跳而去，越过此地的尽头，直走到这里无人知晓的那些原野之上；寻着已经去到人类所居之处的莉拉泽尔（Lirazel）公主，将这道符文交给她，她读罢便一切都会好的。」

===Original===
And the troll scampered thence.

And soon the troll was come with long leaps to the frontier of twilight. Then nothing moved in Elfland any more; and motionless on that splendid throne of which only song may speak sat the old King mourning in silence.

===Chinese===
那巨怪便由此蹦跳而去。

不久那只巨怪便以长长的纵跃来到了暮色的边境。于是精灵国中再无一物移动；那位年老的王静坐在那惟有歌谣方能述说的辉煌宝座之上，默默地哀悼着。

## XXXIII The Shining Line / 三十三 闪耀之线

===Original===
Alveric wandered on, alone of that small company of three without a hope to guide him. For Niv and Zend, who were lately led by the hope of their fantastic quest, no longer yearned for Elfland but were guided now by their plan to hold Alveric back from it. They vacillated more slowly than sane folk, but clung with far more than sane fervour to each vacillation. And Zend that had wandered through so many years with the hope of Elfland before him looked on it, now that he had seen its frontier, as one of the rivals of the moon. Niv who had endured as much for Alveric's quest saw in that magical land something more fabulous than was in all his dreams. And now when Alveric attempted lame cajoleries with those swift and ferocious minds he received no more answers from Zend than the curt statement "It is not the will of the moon": while Niv would only reiterate "Have I not dreams enough?"

===Chinese===
阿尔韦里克继续游荡，在这区区三人的小队里，唯独他再无希望可以指引。因为尼夫和曾德近来本还受着那奇幻远征的希望所引领，如今却不再渴慕精灵国，而是受着另一番谋划的指引——要把阿尔韦里克挡在精灵国之外。他们摇摆起来比清醒之人迟缓得多，可每一次摇摆他们都以远甚于清醒之人的狂热紧紧抱住不放。曾德这许多年来一直以精灵国的希望为前导而漂泊流浪，如今既已亲见其边境，便把它看作月亮的诸多情敌之一。尼夫为阿尔韦里克的远征所承受的并不亚于此，他望见那片充满魔法的国土里有着比他一切梦境都更荒诞离奇的事物。如今阿尔韦里克试图以笨拙的哄骗去应付那些迅疾而凶猛的心智，从曾德处所得的回答无非一句冷冰冰的话：「此非月亮之意」；而尼夫则只会反复重申：「我的梦难道还不够多吗？」

===Original===
They were wandering back again past farms that had known them years before. With their old grey tent more tattered they appeared in the twilight, adding a shade to the evening, in fields wherein they and their tent had become a legend. And never was Alveric unwatched by some mad eye, lest he should slip from the camp and come to Elfland and be where dreams were stranger than Niv's and under a power more magical than the moon.

===Chinese===
他们又游荡着回到了从前认得他们的那些农庄近旁。他们那顶破旧的灰帐篷更加褴褛了，他们在暮色中现身，为傍晚添上一重阴影，置身于那些早已将他们和他们的帐篷当作传说的田野之间。阿尔韦里克无时无刻不在某一只癫狂的眼睛监视之下，唯恐他溜出营帐，奔赴精灵国，去到那些比尼夫之梦更为离奇的梦境所在之处，去到一种比月亮更具魔力的力量之下。

===Original===
Often he tried, creeping silently from his place in the dead of night. One moonlight night he tried first, waiting awake till all the world seemed sleeping. He knew that the frontier was not far away as he crept from the tent into the brightness and black shadows and passed Niv sleeping heavily. A little way he went, and there was Zend sitting still on a rock, gazing into the face of the moon. Round came Zend's face and, newly inspired by the moon, he shouted and leapt at Alveric. They had taken away his sword. And Niv woke and came towards them with immense fury, united to Zend by one jealousy; for each of them knew well that the wonders of Elfland were greater than any fancy that their minds would ever know.

===Chinese===
他屡屡尝试，在夜深人静之时悄然爬起离位。一个月明之夜他头一个试，醒着等候，直到整个世界似乎都已沉睡。当他从帐篷爬入那一片清辉与漆黑的阴影之中、从沉睡沉沉的尼夫身旁经过时，他知道那道边境已不远了。他才行了不远，便见曾德纹丝不动地坐在一块岩石上，凝视着月亮的脸。曾德的脸转了过来，受了月亮新赐的灵感，他大喝一声向阿尔韦里克扑去。他们早已夺去了他的剑。尼夫也醒了，怀着滔天的怒火朝他们走来，与曾德被同一种嫉妒拴在了一处；因为他们每一个人都深知，精灵国里的奇观，远胜过他们的心智所能想象的任何幻景。

===Original===
And again he tried, on a night when no moon shone. But on that night Niv was sitting outside the camp, relishing in a strange and joyless way a certain comradeship that there was between his ravings and the interstellar darkness. And there in the night he saw Alveric slipping away towards the land whose wonders far transcended all Niv's poor dreams; and all the fury the lesser can feel for the greater awoke at once in his mind; and, creeping up behind him, without any help from Zend he smote Alveric insensible to the ground.

===Chinese===
他又试了一回，在一个无月之夜。可那一夜尼夫正坐在营外，以一种奇异而无欢的方式品味着他那狂言呓语与星辰之间的幽暗之间所存在的一种同伴之谊。就在那夜里，他看见阿尔韦里克正悄悄地朝那片国土溜去——那国土的奇观远胜过尼夫所有寒酸的梦；凡是渺小者对伟大者所能感到的一切怒火，顿时在他心头一齐觉醒；他悄悄从背后摸上去，无须曾德相助，一拳将阿尔韦里克打得不省人事，扑倒在地。

===Original===
And never did Alveric plan any escape after that but that the busy thoughts of madness anticipated it.

===Chinese===
自此之后，阿尔韦里克每筹谋任何一次逃脱，癫狂那纷忙的念头总抢先他一步。

===Original===
And so they came, watchers and watched, over the fields of men. And Alveric sought help of the folk of the farms; but the cunning of Niv knew too well the tricks of sanity. So that when the folk came running over their fields to that queer grey tent from which they heard Alveric's cries, they found Niv and Zend posed in a calm that they had much practised, while Alveric told of his thwarted quest of Elfland. Now by many men all quests are considered mad, as the cunning of Niv knew. Alveric found no help here.

===Chinese===
他们就这样，监视者与被监视者一同，越过了人世的田野。阿尔韦里克向那些农庄的人求援；可尼夫的狡黠对清醒之人的一切花招了如指掌。于是当那些农人闻阿尔韦里克的呼喊声而奔过自家田野、来到那顶古怪的灰色帐篷跟前时，他们所见的，是尼夫和曾德摆出一副久经演练的镇定，而阿尔韦里克正在述说他那受挫的精灵国之征。世间本就有许多人把一切远征都视作癫狂——这一点尼夫的狡黠心知肚明。阿尔韦里克于此地一无所获。

===Original===
As they went back by the way by which they had marched for years Niv led that band of three, stepping ahead of Alveric and Zend with his lean face held high, made all the leaner by the long thin points to which he had trained his beard and his moustaches, and wearing Alveric's sword that stuck out long behind him and its hilt high in front. And he stepped and perked his head with a certain air that revealed to the rare travellers who saw him that this sparse and ragged figure esteemed itself the leader of a greater band than were visible. Indeed if one had just seen him at the end of the evening with the dusk and the mist of the fenlands close behind him he might have believed that in the dusk and the mist was an army that followed this gay worn confident man. Had the army been there Niv was sane. Had the world accepted that an army was there, even though only Alveric and Zend followed his curious steps, still he was sane. But the lonely fancy that had not fact to feed on, nor the fancy of any other for fellowship, was for its loneliness mad.

===Chinese===
他们沿着多年行军所走过的旧路折返，尼夫领着这三人小队，走在阿尔韦里克与曾德之前，将他那张瘦脸高高昂起，那脸因他把胡须和髭须蓄成长长尖尖的形状而显得愈发瘦削，身上挎着阿尔韦里克的剑，剑身在他身后长长地戳出，剑柄则在身前高高翘起。他迈着步子，神情倨傲地把头一挺，那副气派向偶然遇见他的稀客透露：这一副稀疏褴褛的身影，自视为一支远比目所能见者更为庞大的队伍的首领。倘若有人恰在傍晚尽头看见他，薄暮与沼泽的雾气紧贴在他身后，那人也许会以为，那薄暮与雾气之中正有一支大军，追随在这位神气十足、饱经风霜、满心自信的人身后。倘若那支大军当真存在，尼夫便是清醒的。倘若世人接受有一支大军存在——纵然其实只有阿尔韦里克和曾德跟着他那古怪的步履——他也仍是清醒的。然而这一桩孤独的幻想，既无事实可资滋养，又无他人的幻想与之结伴同行，正因为它的孤独，才是癫狂的。

===Original===
Zend watched Alveric all the while, as they marched behind Niv; for their mutual jealousy of the wonders of Elfland bound Niv and Zend together to work as with one wild whim.

===Chinese===
他们随在尼夫身后行进时，曾德始终盯着阿尔韦里克；因为他们彼此对精灵国奇观的同一种嫉妒，把尼夫和曾德紧紧拴在一处，使他们如同一桩狂放的执念般协同行事。

===Original===
And now one morning Niv stretched himself up to the fullest possible height of his lean inches and extended his right arm high and addressed his army, "We are come near again to Erl," he said. "And we shall bring new fancies in place of outworn things and things that are stale; and its customs shall be henceforth the way of the moon."

===Chinese===
于是在一个清晨，尼夫将他那瘦骨嶙峋的身躯挺到了所能达到的极致高度，高高伸出右臂，对他的大军训话：「我们又一次临近厄尔了，」他说，「我们要以新的玄想替代那些陈旧之物、那些腐朽之物；它的风俗从此以后便是月亮之道。」

===Original===
Now Niv cared nothing for the moon, but he had great cunning, and he knew that Zend would aid his new plan against Erl if only for the sake of the moon. And Zend cheered till the echoes came back from a lonely hill, and Niv smiled to them like a leader confident of his hosts. And Alveric rose against them then, and struggled with Niv and Zend for the last time, and learned that age or wandering or loss of hope had left him unable to strive against the maniacal strength of these two. And after that he went with them more meekly, with resignation, caring no longer what befell him, living only in memory and only for days that had been; and in November evenings in this dim camp in the chill he saw, looking only backwards through the years, spring mornings shine again on the towers of Erl. In the light of these mornings he saw Orion again, playing again with old toys that the witch had made with a spell; he saw Lirazel move once more through the gracious gardens. Yet no light that memory is able to kindle was strong enough to illumine much that camp in those sombre evenings, when the damp rose up from the ground and the chill swooped out of the air, and Niv and Zend as darkness came stealing nearer began to chatter in low eager voices schemes inspired by such whims as throve at dusk in the waste. Only when the sad day drifted wholly thence and Alveric slept by flapping tatters that streamed from the tent in the night, then only was memory, unhindered by the busy changes of day, able to bring back Erl to him, bright, happy and vernal; so that while his body lay still, in far fields, in the dark and the winter, all that was most active and live in him was back over the wolds in Erl, back over the years in spring with Lirazel and Orion.

===Chinese===
其实尼夫对月亮毫不在意，可他极为狡黠，深知只要为着月亮的缘故，曾德便会助成他对付厄尔的新谋划。于是曾德欢呼喝彩，直到孤丘之上也传来回声，尼夫则像一位对自己的大军满怀信心的统帅那般对他们微笑。阿尔韦里克那时便奋起反抗他们，与尼夫和曾德作了最后一搏，这才明白：年岁，或漂泊，或希望的丧失，已使他无力再与这两人癫狂的大力相抗。自那以后他便更为驯顺地随他们而去，怀着一种听天由命，不再在乎自己将遭逢什么，只活在记忆之中，只为那些已逝的日子而活；在十一月的傍晚，在这寒气侵人的昏暗营地里，他只往岁月的来路回望，看见了春日的清晨重又照耀在厄尔的塔楼之上。在那一片清晨的光里，他又看见了奥利安，重新玩着女巫以咒语做成的旧玩具；他看见莉拉泽尔重又在那些优雅的花园里穿行。然而记忆所能点燃的任何光亮，都不足以照亮那些阴沉傍晚里的那座营地——彼时湿气从地上升腾，寒意从空气中扑袭而下，尼夫和曾德随着夜色悄然逼近，开始压低了嗓子、迫不及待地喋喋低语，谋划着那些为荒原上薄暮之中滋长的种种执念所激的诡计。唯有当那哀愁的一日彻底从此地飘散、阿尔韦里克伴着夜里从帐篷里猎猎飘扬的破布条入睡之时，唯有那时，记忆才能不受白日纷忙变幻的牵绊，把厄尔带回到他身边，明亮、欢乐、春意盎然；于是当他的身躯静静躺着，卧在遥远的田野间、卧在黑暗与严冬之中时，他身上一切最为活跃、最为蓬勃的部分，都已回到了丘陵那一边的厄尔，回到了岁月那一边的春天，与莉拉泽尔和奥利安同在。

===Original===
How far he was bodily, in sheer miles from his home, for which his happy thoughts each night forsook his weary frame, Alveric knew not. It was many years since their tent had stood one evening a grey shape in that landscape in which it now waved its tatters. But Niv knew that of late they had come nearer to Erl, for his dreams of it came to him now soon after he fell asleep, and they used to come to him further on in the night, on the other side of midnight and even towards morning: and from this he argued that they used to have further to come, and were now but a little way off. When he told this secretly one evening to Zend, Zend listened gravely but gave no opinion, merely saying "The moon knows." Nevertheless he followed Niv, who led this curious caravan always in that direction from which his dreams of the valley of Erl came soonest. And this queer leadership brought them nearer to Erl, as often happens where men follow leaders that are crazy or blind or deceived; they reach some port or other though they stray down the years with little foresight enough: were it otherwise what would become of us?

===Chinese===
他肉身究竟离故土有多远——以纯然的英里计——而那故土却是他每夜快乐的思绪所抛弃这副疲倦皮囊奔赴之所——阿尔韦里克并不知道。自他们的帐篷曾在某个傍晚作为一种灰色的轮廓矗立于那片风景之中，至今已有许多年了；而如今它在同一片风景中飘扬着它的破布条。可尼夫知道近来他们已更近厄尔，因为他梦见它的时辰如今已在他入睡后不久便到来，而从前那些梦要来得更晚一些，在午夜那一边，甚至将近清晨：他据此推论，那些梦从前要走的路更远，而如今只差一点点路了。某天傍晚他把这事悄悄告诉曾德，曾德一脸肃然地听着，却不置可否，只说：「月亮知道。」然而他仍跟着尼夫，尼夫领着这支古怪的商队，始终朝着他梦见厄尔之谷来得最快的那个方向走。而这般古怪的引领，竟把他们带得离厄尔越来越近，正如人们在追随癫狂、盲目或受蒙蔽的领袖时常常发生的情形：他们虽一路漂流岁月、所具的远见寥寥无几，却终究会抵达某个港口或别的归宿；倘若不然，我们又当如何呢？

===Original===
And one day the upper parts of the towers of Erl looked at them out of blue distance, shining in early sunlight above a curve of the downs. And towards them Niv turned at once and led directly, for the line of their wandering march had not pointed straight to Erl, and marched on as a conqueror that sees some new city's gates. What his plans were Alveric did not know, but kept to his apathy; and Zend did not know, for Niv had merely said that his plans must be secret; nor did Niv know, for his fancies poured through his brain and rushed away; what fancies made what plans in a mood that was yesterday's how could he tell today?

===Chinese===
终于有一天，厄尔塔楼的上半截从一片蓝色的远处向他们凝望，在晨光里闪耀，浮于丘陵的一道弧线之上。尼夫立刻朝着它们转过身去，径直引领前行——因为他们一路漂泊的行军路线原本并未直指厄尔——他像一位望见某座新城池之门的征服者那样向前行进。他的计划是什么，阿尔韦里克并不知道，只是依旧沉浸在他的漠然之中；曾德也不知道，因为尼夫只说他的计划必须保密；尼夫自己也不知道，因为种种玄想在他的脑中倾泻而过、奔涌而去；昨日某种心境里是哪一种玄想构成了哪一种计划，他今日又怎能说清？

===Original===
Then as they went they soon came to a shepherd, standing amongst his grazing sheep and leaning upon his crook, who watched and seemed to have no other care but only to watch all things going by, or, when nothing passed, to gaze and gaze at the downs till all his memories were fashioned out of their huge grass curves. He stood, a bearded man, and watched them with never a word as they passed. And one of Niv's mad memories suddenly knew him, and Niv hailed him by his name and the shepherd answered. And who should he be but Vand!

===Chinese===
他们走着走着，不久便遇见一个牧羊人，站在他放牧的羊群之中，倚着他的牧羊杖，望着，似乎再无别的牵挂，只关心看着一切经过的事物，或当一无所有经过时，便久久凝望那些丘陵，直望到他全部的记忆都由那些巨大的青草弧线所塑成。他站着，一个蓄着胡须的男子，在他们经过时一言不发地望着他们。尼夫那一堆癫狂的记忆里，忽然有一条认出了他，尼夫便喊出了他的名字，那牧羊人也应声作答。他不是别人，正是范德！

===Original===
Then they fell talking; and Niv spoke suavely, as he always did with sane folk, aping with clever mimicry the ways and the tricks of sanity, lest Alveric should ask for help against him. But Alveric sought no help. Silent he stood and heard the others talking, but his thoughts were far in the past and their voices were only sounds to him. And Vand enquired of them if they had found Elfland. But he spoke as one asks of children if their toy boat has been to the Happy Isles. He had had for many years to do with sheep, and had come to know their needs and their price, and the need men have of them; and these things had risen imperceptibly up all round his imagination, and were at last a wall over which he saw no further. When he was young, yes once, he had sought for Elfland; but now, why now he was older; such things were for the young.

===Chinese===
于是他们攀谈起来；尼夫言辞温婉，一如他向来对待清醒之人那样，以机巧的摹拟学尽清醒之人的作派与花招，唯恐阿尔韦里克开口求人助己对付他。可阿尔韦里克并未求援。他默默站着，听旁人说话，心思却远在从前，他们的声音于他不过是些声响罢了。范德问他们可曾寻见精灵国。可他说话的口气，就像人问孩子们他们的玩具小船可曾到过极乐之岛。多年来他与羊为伍，已熟知它们的脾性与价值，以及人对它们的需要；这些事物不知不觉地在他想象力的四周一重重升起，终于成了一道墙，他越过这道墙再也看不见更远。年轻的时候，是的，有那么一回，他也曾寻访过精灵国；可如今——唉，如今他老了；这些事是属于年轻人的。

===Original===
"But we saw its border," said Zend, "the border of twilight."

"A mist," said Vand, "of the evening."

"I have stood," said Zend, "upon the edge of Elfland."

But Vand smiled and shook his bearded head as he leaned on his long crook, and every wave of his beard as he shook it slowly denied Zend's tales of that border, and his lips smiled it away, and his tolerant eyes were grave with the lore of the fields we know.

"No, not Elfland," he said.

===Chinese===
「可我们看见了它的边境，」曾德说，「那暮色的边境。」

「一层雾，」范德说，「傍晚的雾。」

「我曾站在，」曾德说，「精灵国的边缘之上。」

可范德微微一笑，摇了摇他那长着胡须的头，他倚在那根长长的牧羊杖上，每摇一下，他的胡须便缓缓地否认着曾德关于那道边境的种种说法，他的唇边带着笑把它一笔勾销，他那双宽容的眼睛，却因饱谙我们所知原野的掌故而肃穆深沉。

「不，那不是精灵国。」他说。

===Original===
And Niv agreed with Vand, for he watched his mood, studying the ways of sanity. And they spoke of Elfland lightly, as one tells of some dream that came at dawn and went away before waking. And Alveric heard with despair, for Lirazel dwelt not only over the border but even, as he saw now, beyond human belief; so that all at once she seemed remoter than ever, and he still lonelier.

"I sought for it once," said Vand, "but no, there's no Elfland."

"No," said Niv, and only Zend wondered.

"No," replied Vand and shook his head and lifted his eyes to his sheep.

===Chinese===
尼夫也附和范德，因为他在留心他的神色，研习着清醒之人的作派。他们轻描淡写地谈起精灵国，就像人讲述某个在黎明降生、未及醒来便已消散的梦。阿尔韦里克怀着绝望听着，因为莉拉泽尔所栖身的，不仅在那道边境之外，甚至——如他此刻才看清——已越出了人所能信的范围之外；于是她忽地显得比以往任何时候都更为遥远，而他也愈发孤独。

「我曾寻过它一回，」范德说，「可不，根本没有精灵国。」

「没有。」尼夫说，唯有曾德暗暗称奇。

「没有。」范德答道，摇了摇头，抬眼望向他的羊群。

===Original===
And just beyond his sheep and coming towards them he saw a shining line. So long his eyes stayed fixed on that shining line coming over the downs from the eastward that the others turned and looked.

They saw it too, a shimmering line of silver, or a little blue like steel, flickering and changing with the reflection of strange passing colours. And before it, very faint like threatening breezes breathing before a storm, came the soft sound of very old songs. It caught, as they all stood gazing, one of Vand's furthest sheep; and instantly its fleece was that pure gold that is told of in old romance; and the shining line came on and the sheep disappeared altogether. They saw now that it was about the height of the mist from a small stream; and still Vand stood gazing at it, neither moving nor thinking. But Niv turned very soon and beckoned curtly to Zend and seized Alveric by the arm and hastened away towards Erl. The gleaming line, that seemed to bump and stumble over every unevenness of the rough fields, came not so fast as they hastened; yet it never stopped when they rested, never wearied when they were tired, but came on over all the hills and hedges of Earth; nor did sunset change its appearance or check its pace.

===Chinese===
就在他的羊群的那一边，正朝他们而来，他望见了一道闪耀之线。他目不转睛地盯着那道从丘陵之上自东方而来的闪耀之线，看得那么久，连旁的人也都转过身去看。

他们也看见了，一条闪烁的银线，或者带点钢铁那样的微蓝，随着奇异的流光掠过而闪烁变幻。在它之前，有极轻柔的、十分古老的歌声传来，微弱得犹如风暴将至前喘息的威胁之风。就在他们都站着凝望之际，它卷住了范德最远处的一只羊；刹那之间，那羊的羊毛便成了古老传奇中所传的那种纯金；那道闪耀之线继续前行，那只羊便整个地消失了。他们这才看清，它的高度大约相当于一条小溪上升起的薄雾；而范德仍旧站在那里凝望着它，既不动，也不想。尼夫却很快就转过身，向曾德简短地一招手，抓住阿尔韦里克的胳膊，匆匆朝厄尔而去。那道闪闪发亮的线，似乎在粗砺田野的每一处高低不平之处都颠簸磕绊，却不及他们赶路那般快；然而他们歇脚时它从不停止，他们疲倦时它从不疲倦，只顾越过了人世间一切的丘陵与树篱前行；日落也不能改变它的模样，不能阻住它的步伐。

## XII The Unenchanted Plain / 十二 失去魔法的原野

===Original===
When Alveric understood that he had lost Elfland it was already evening and he had been gone two days and a night from Erl. For the second time he lay down for the night on that shingly plain whence Elfland had ebbed away: and at sunset the eastern horizon showed clear against turquoise sky, all black and jagged with rocks, without any sign of Elfland. And the twilight glimmered, but it was Earth’s twilight, and not that dense barrier for which Alveric looked, which lies between Elfland and Earth. And the stars came out and were the stars we know, and Alveric slept below their familiar constellations.

He awoke in the birdless dawn very cold, hearing old voices crying faintly far off, as they slowly drifted away, like dreams going back to dreamland. He wondered if they would come to Elfland again, or if Elfland had ebbed too far. He searched all the horizon eastwards, and still saw nothing but the rocks of that desolate land. So he turned again toward the fields we know.

===Chinese===
阿尔韦里克明白自己已经失去精灵国时，天色已晚，他离开厄尔已有两天一夜。他第二度在那片碎石遍布的原野上躺下过夜——精灵国正是从那里退却而去的：日落时分，东方的天际在绿松石色的天空下清晰可辨，黑压压地满是嶙峋乱石，不见精灵国的丝毫踪影。暮霭闪烁，却是人间的暮霭，并不是阿尔韦里克所寻的那道横亘于精灵国与人间之间的浓密屏障。群星出来了，是我们所熟识的星辰，阿尔韦里克便在那些熟悉的星座之下睡去。

他在没有鸟啼的黎明中醒来，冻得直打哆嗦，隐约听见古老的歌谣在远方微弱地回荡，正缓缓飘散，仿佛梦境归回梦乡。他不知道它们还会不会再度来到精灵国，抑或精灵国已经退得太远。他向东细细搜寻整道天际，所见的仍是那片荒原上的乱石，别无他物。于是他转身又朝我们所知的那些原野走去。

===Original===
He walked back through the cold with all his impatience gone; and gradually some warmth came to him from walking, and later a little from the autumnal sun. He walked all day, and the sun was growing huge and red when he came again to the leather-worker’s cottage. He asked for food, and the old man made him welcome: his pot was already simmering for his own evening meal: and it was not long before Alveric was sitting at the old table before a dish full of squirrels’ legs, hedgehogs and rabbit’s meat. The old man would not eat till Alveric had eaten, but waited on him with such solicitude that Alveric felt that the moment of his opportunity was come, and turned to the old man as he offered him a piece of the back of a rabbit, and approached the subject of Elfland.

===Chinese===
他顶着寒冷往回走，那一股急切之心已消散殆尽；走着走着，身上渐渐有了些暖意，其后秋日的太阳又添了些许温热。他走了一整天，待到再度来到皮匠的小屋前时，太阳已变得又大又红。他讨要食物，老人殷勤相待：他那口锅本就为着自己的晚饭在那里炖着：没过多久，阿尔韦里克便坐在那张旧桌前，面前一盘盛满了松鼠腿、刺猬和兔肉。老人非要等阿尔韦里克吃罢才肯动筷，却如此殷勤地在一旁伺候，使阿尔韦里克觉得良机已至，便在老人递给他一块兔脊肉时转向他，将话题引到了精灵国上。

===Original===
“The twilight is further away,” said Alveric.

“Yes, yes,” said the old man without any meaning in his voice, whatever he had in his mind.

“When did it go?” said Alveric.

“The twilight, master?” said his host.

“Yes,” said Alveric.

“Ah, the twilight,” the old man said.

“The barrier,” said Alveric, and he lowered his voice, although he knew not why, “between here and Elfland.”

At the word Elfland all comprehension faded out of the old man’s eyes.

“Ah,” he said.

“Old man,” said Alveric, “you know where Elfland has gone.”

“Gone?” said the old man.

===Chinese===
「那暮色更远了。」阿尔韦里克说。

「是啊，是啊。」老人说，话音里听不出半点意味，无论他心里想着什么。

「它什么时候走的？」阿尔韦里克问。

「暮色吗，少爷？」他的主人问。

「是啊。」阿尔韦里克说。

「啊，暮色。」老人说。

「那道屏障，」阿尔韦里克说，不知不觉放低了声音，虽则他自己也不知为何，「在这人间与精灵国之间的那道。」

一听精灵国这三个字，老人眼中那一丝了然的神色便尽数褪去。

「啊。」他说。

「老人家，」阿尔韦里克说，「你知道精灵国上哪儿去了。」

「去了？」老人说。

===Original===
That innocent surprise, thought Alveric, must be real; but at least he knew where it had been; it used to be only two fields away from his door.

“Elfland was in the next field once,” said Alveric.

And the old man’s eyes roved back into the past, and he gazed as it were on old days awhile, then he shook his head. And Alveric fixed him with his eye.

“You knew Elfland,” he exclaimed.

Still the old man did not answer.

“You knew where the border was,” said Alveric.

“I am old,” said the leather-worker, “and I have no one to ask.”

When he said that, Alveric knew that he was thinking of his old wife, and he knew too that had she been alive and standing there at that moment yet he would have had no news of Elfland: there seemed little more to say. But a certain petulance held him to the subject after he knew it to be hopeless.

===Chinese===
阿尔韦里克心想，那份天真的惊讶想必不是装出来的；可他至少该知道精灵国原先在何处；它从前不过离他门口两块田远。

「精灵国从前就在隔壁那块田里。」阿尔韦里克说。

老人的目光便游回到了往昔之中，仿佛凝望了一会旧日时光，随即摇了摇头。阿尔韦里克拿眼睛定定地盯住他。

「你是知道精灵国的。」他叫道。

老人仍不作答。

「你是知道边境在哪里的。」阿尔韦里克说。

「我老了，」皮匠说，「又无人可问。」

听他这话，阿尔韦里克便知道他想的是他那故去的老妻，他也明白纵使她还活着、此刻就站在那里，他也照样打听不到精灵国的消息：仿佛再没什么可说的了。可一股执拗的意气却在他明知无望之后，仍把他拴在这话题上。

===Original===
“Who lives to the east of here?” he said.

“To the east?” the old man replied. “Master, are there not north and south and west that you needs must look to the east?”

There was a look of entreaty in his face but Alveric did not heed it. “Who lives to the east?” he said.

“Master, no one lives to the east,” he answered. And that indeed was true.

“What used to be there?” said Alveric.

And the old man turned away to see to the stewing of his pot, and muttered as he turned, so that one hardly heard him.

“The past,” he said.

===Chinese===
「这儿的东边住着谁？」他说。

「东边？」老人答道，「少爷，不是还有北、还有南、还有西么，何苦非要朝东看呢？」

他脸上有一种恳求的神色，可阿尔韦里克不曾理会。「东边住着谁？」他又问。

「少爷，东边没有人住。」他答道。这话倒也不假。

「从前那儿有什么？」阿尔韦里克说。

老人便转身去看他那口炖着的锅，一边转身边咕哝着，声音低得几乎听不见。

「往昔。」他说。

===Original===
No more would the old man say, nor explain what he had said. So Alveric asked him if he could have a bed for the night, and his host showed him the old bed he remembered across that vague number of years. And Alveric accepted the bed without more ado so as to let the old man go to his own supper. And very soon Alveric was deep asleep, warm and resting at last, while his host turned over slowly in his mind many things of which Alveric had supposed he knew nothing.

===Chinese===
老人再不肯多言，也不肯解释他方才所说的话。于是阿尔韦里克问他能否在此借宿一宵，主人便领他到了那张他隔着那许多年仍记得的旧床前。阿尔韦里克也不再推辞，径自受下，好让老人去吃自己的晚饭。不多时阿尔韦里克便沉沉睡去，终于暖和了，也休息了，而他的主人却在心里慢慢地翻来覆去地想着许多事——阿尔韦里克原以为他对这些事一无所知。

===Original===
When the birds of our fields woke Alveric, singing late in the last of October, on a morning that reminded them of spring, he rose and went out of doors, and went to the highest part of the little field that lay on the windowless side of the old man’s house toward Elfland. There he looked eastward and saw all the way to the curved line of the sky the same barren, desolate, rocky plain that had been there yesterday and the day before. Then the leather-worker gave him breakfast, and afterwards he went out and looked again at the plain. And over his dinner, which his host timidly shared, Alveric neared once more the subject of Elfland. And something in the old man’s sayings or silences made Alveric hopeful that even yet he would have some news of the whereabouts of the pale-blue Elfin Mountains. So he brought the old man out and turned to the east, to which his companion looked with reluctant eyes; and pointing to one particular rock, the most noticeable and near, said, hoping for definite news of a definite thing, “How long has that rock been there?”

And the answer came to his hopes like hail to apple-blossom: “It is there and we must make the best of it.”

===Chinese===
当我们原野上的鸟雀唤醒阿尔韦里克时——它们在十月末的最后几天里还唱着迟来的歌，那一个早晨使它们想起春日——他便起身走出门去，走到老人屋子那没有窗子、朝着精灵国的一侧的那块小田里最高的地方。他在那里向东眺望，一直望到天穹那道弧线，所见的仍是昨日和前日便在的那片荒芜、凄凉、乱石嶙峋的原野。随后皮匠请他吃了早饭，之后他又出门再去看看那片原野。到了午饭时分，主人怯怯地与他同食，阿尔韦里克再度将话题挨近精灵国。老人或是言语、或是沉默中的某种东西，使阿尔韦里克生出一丝希望，觉得说不定仍能打听到那淡蓝色的精灵山脉的下落。于是他把老人带到屋外，转身朝向东方，他的同伴则以不愿正视的目光望着那边；阿尔韦里克指着一块石头——那最显眼、最近的一块——指望着对一件确切之物打听到确切的消息，说道：「那块石头在那儿多久了？」

那回答却如冰雹砸落苹果花一般落在他的期望上：「它就在那儿，咱们只好将就着过了。」

===Original===
The unexpectedness of the answer dazed Alveric; and when he saw that reasonable questions about definite things brought him no logical answer he despaired of getting practical information to guide his fantastic journey. So he walked on the eastward side of the cottage all the afternoon, watching the dreary plain, and it never changed or moved: no pale-blue mountains appeared, no Elfland came flooding back: and evening came and the rocks glowed dully with the low rays of the sun, and darkened when it set, changing with all Earth’s changes, but with no enchantment of Elfland. Then Alveric decided on a great journey.

He returned to the cottage and told the leather-worker that he needed to buy much provisions, as much as he could carry. And over supper they planned what he should have. And the old man promised to go next day amongst the neighbours; telling of all the things he would get from each, and somewhat more if God should prosper his snaring. For Alveric had determined to travel eastward till he found the lost land.

===Chinese===
这回答之出乎意料，令阿尔韦里克一时怔住；待他明白对确切之物提出合理的问题却换不来合乎逻辑的回答，便对能为这场奇幻之旅寻得切实指引一事死了心。于是他整个下午都在小屋东侧踱步，望着那片沉闷的原野，它从不变化、也不稍动：没有淡蓝色的山脉浮现，没有精灵国如潮水般涌回：傍晚来临，乱石在低斜的阳光中发出黯淡的红光，日落时又暗了下去，随人间的一切变迁而变迁，却无精灵国的一丝魔法。于是阿尔韦里克下定决心，要作一次远行。

他回到小屋，告诉皮匠他需要购买大批干粮，越多越好，只求他能背得动。他们便在晚饭时商量好他该备些什么。老人答应次日去邻里之间走动一番；一一说出他要从各家讨到些什么，倘若上帝保佑他下的套子有所收获，还能再多弄到一些。因为阿尔韦里克已下定决心要一路东行，直到寻见那片失去的土地。

===Original===
And Alveric slept early, and slept long, till the last of his fatigue was gone which came from his pursuit of Elfland: the old man woke him as he came back from his snaring. And the creatures that he had snared the old man put in his pot and hung it over his fire, while Alveric ate his breakfast. And all the morning the leather-worker went from house to house amongst his neighbours, dwelling on little farms at the edge of the fields we know; and he got salted meats from some, bread from one, a cheese from another, and came back burdened to his house in time to prepare dinner.

And all the provender that burdened the old man Alveric shouldered in a sack, and some he put in his wallet; and he filled his water-bottle and two more besides that his host had made from large skins, for he had seen no streams at all in the desolate land; and thus equipped he walked some way from the cottage, and looked again at the land from which Elfland had ebbed. He came back satisfied that he could carry provisions for a fortnight.

===Chinese===
阿尔韦里克早早睡下，睡了很久，直到追猎精灵国所带来的最后一丝疲乏也消散净尽：老人下完套子回来时才把他唤醒。老人把套来的那些小兽放进锅里，悬在火上，阿尔韦里克则在一旁吃他的早饭。整整一上午，皮匠在我们所知的原野边缘那些小田庄上挨家挨户地走动；他从这家讨得些腌肉，从那家讨得面包，又从另一家讨得一块奶酪，赶回家里做午饭时已背得沉甸甸的。

老人背负的那许多口粮，阿尔韦里克统统装进一只口袋扛上肩，又把一些塞进背袋；他灌满了自己的水壶，还另灌了两只主人用大块皮子缝制的水囊，因为在那片荒原上他压根儿没见过一条溪流；如此装备停当，他走出小屋一段路，又回头望了望那片精灵国退去后留下的土地。他走回来时心下踏实，知道自己能带上够吃半个月的干粮上路。

===Original===
And in the evening while the old man prepared pieces of squirrels’ meat Alveric stood again on the windowless side of the cottage, gazing still across the lonely land, hoping always to see emerge from the clouds that were colouring at sunset, those serene pale-blue mountains; and seeing never a peak. And the sun set, and that was the last of October.

Next morning Alveric made a good meal in the cottage; then took his heavy burden of provisions, and paid his host and started. The door of the cottage opened toward the west and the old man cordially saw him away from his door with godspeed and farewells, but he would not move round his house to watch him going eastward; nor would he speak of that journey: it was as though to him there were only three points of the compass.

===Chinese===
到了傍晚，老人在那儿切弄松鼠肉块，阿尔韦里克又站到小屋没有窗子的那一侧，依旧凝望着那片寂寥的土地，一心盼着能看见那些宁谧的淡蓝色山脉从落日染红的云层中浮现出来；却始终看不见一座峰影。太阳落下去了，那一天是十月的最后一日。

次日清晨，阿尔韦里克在小屋里饱餐了一顿；然后背起沉重的干粮，付了主人钱，便动身上路。小屋的门朝西开，老人殷勤地送他出门，说着一路平安与珍重的话，却不肯绕过屋子去看他往东走的样子；也不肯提起那一趟旅程：仿佛在他看来，罗盘上只有三个方位。

===Original===
The bright autumnal sun was not yet high when Alveric went from the fields we know to the land that Elfland had left and that nothing else went near, with his big sack over his shoulder and his sword at his side. The May trees of memory that he had seen were all withered now, and the old songs and voices that had haunted that land were all now faint as sighs; and there seemed to be fewer of them, as though some had already died or had struggled back to Elfland.

All that day Alveric travelled, with the vigour that waits at the beginning of journeys, which helped him on though he was burdened with so much provisions, and a big blanket that he wore like a heavy cloak round his shoulders; and he carried besides a bundle of firewood, and a stave in his right hand. He was an incongruous figure with his stave and his sack and his sword; but he followed one idea, one inspiration, one hope; and so shared something of the strangeness that all men have who do this.

===Chinese===
明晃晃的秋日还没升高，阿尔韦里克便从我们所知的原野走进了那片精灵国舍弃后、再无他物肯靠近的土地，肩上扛着大口袋，腰间佩着剑。他记忆中所见过的那些五月树如今都已枯萎，曾经在那片土地上萦回的古老歌谣与声音，如今都微弱得如同一声叹息；而且似乎也少了许多，仿佛有些已经死去，又仿佛有些已挣扎着回到了精灵国。

那一整天阿尔韦里克都在赶路，凭着一股行旅之初才有的那股劲儿，虽然背负着那么多干粮，又裹着一条当作沉重斗篷披在肩上的大毯子，这股劲头仍推着他前行；他此外还带着一捆柴薪，右手握着一根手杖。带着手杖、口袋和佩剑，他这副模样颇不协调；可他只追随一个念头、一股灵感、一线希望；凡是这样的人，身上都或多或少带着一种奇异的气质，他也如此。

===Original===
Halting at noon to eat and rest he went slowly on again and walked till evening: even then he did not rest as he had intended, for when twilight fell and lay heavy along the eastern sky he continually rose from his resting and went a little further to see if it might not be that dense deep twilight that made the frontier of the fields we know, shutting them off from Elfland. But it was always earthly twilight, until the stars came out, and they were all the familiar stars that look on Earth. Then he lay down among those unrounded and mossless rocks, and ate bread and cheese and drank water; and as the cold of night began to come over the plain he lit a small fire with his scanty bundle of wood and lay close to it with his cloak and his blanket round him; and before the embers were black he was sound asleep.

Dawn came without sound of bird or whisper of leaves or grasses, dawn came in dead silence and cold; and nothing on all that plain gave a welcome back to the light.

===Chinese===
中午他停下吃点东西歇一歇，又缓缓上路，一直走到傍晚：即便那时他也不曾如他所打算的那样歇下，因为当暮色降临、沉沉地压在东方的天际上时，他便一次次从歇处起身，再往前走上一程，去看看那会不会是那浓重深沉的暮色——它构成我们所知原野的边境，将它们与精灵国隔绝。可那始终是人间的暮色，直到群星出来，而它们尽是我们所熟识的、照临人间的那些星辰。于是他在那些既不圆浑、又无苔藓的乱石之间躺下，吃了些面包和奶酪，喝了些水；当夜的寒意开始漫过原野，他用那点为数不多的柴薪生了一小堆火，裹着斗篷和毯子紧紧挨着火躺下；余烬还没全黑，他便已沉沉睡去。

黎明来了，没有一声鸟啼，没有一片树叶或一根草的低语，黎明在死一般的寂静与寒冷中降临；那整片原野上，没有一物对重新到来的光明表示欢迎。

===Original===
If darkness had lain forever upon those angular rocks it were better, Alveric thought, as he saw their shapeless companies sullenly glowing; darkness were better now that Elfland was gone. And though the misery of that disenchanted place entered his spirit with the chill of the dawn, yet his fiery hope still shone, and gave him little time to eat by the cold black circle of his lonely fire before it hurried him onward easterly over the rocks. And all that morning he travelled on without the comradeship of a blade of grass. The golden birds that he had seen before had long since fled back to Elfland, and the birds of our fields and all living things we know shunned all that empty waste. Alveric travelled as much alone as a man who goes back in memory to revisit remembered scenes, and instead of remembered scenes he was in a place from which every glamour had gone. He travelled somewhat lighter than on the day before, but he went more wearily, for he felt more heavily now the fatigue of the previous day. He rested long at midday and then went on. The myriad rocks stretched on and slightly jagged the horizon, and all day there came no glimpse of the pale-blue mountains. That evening from his dwindling provision of wood Alveric made another fire; its little flame going up alone in that waste seemed somehow to reveal the monstrous loneliness. He sat by his fire and thought of Lirazel and would not give up hope, though a glance at those rocks might have warned him not to hope, for something in their chaotic look partook of the plain that bred them, and they hinted it to be infinite.

===Chinese===
倘若永远的黑暗笼罩着这些棱角峥嵘的乱石，倒还更好些——阿尔韦里克望着它们那一簇簇不成形的身影阴郁地泛着光，心想；精灵国既已离去，黑暗倒还更好。虽说那片被剥夺了魔法的处所之凄凉，随着黎明的寒气一同侵入了他的心神，可他那炽热的希望仍闪闪不灭，只让他在那堆孤零零的火所余的冷而黑的灰圈边匆匆吃了几口东西，便催着他向东越过乱石继续前行。那一整个上午他都在赶路，身边连一根草作伴都没有。他从前所见过的那些金色鸟儿早已飞回精灵国去了，我们原野上的鸟雀、以及我们所知的一切活物，都远远避开那片空旷的荒地。阿尔韦里克走得如此孤独，宛如一个人循着记忆回去重访旧日的光景，可他所到的并非记忆中的旧景，而是一个一切神魅都已散尽的地方。他比前一日走得轻了些，却走得更为倦怠，因为前一天的疲劳此刻更沉重地压在他身上。中午他歇了很久，然后再继续走。无数的乱石向前延伸，微微参差地割裂着天际，整整一天都不见那淡蓝色山脉的一丝影子。那天傍晚，阿尔韦里克用所剩无几的柴薪又生了一堆火；那点小火焰孤零零地在荒原中升起，竟似反而衬出了那骇人的孤寂。他坐在火边，想念着莉拉泽尔，不肯放弃希望，纵使看一眼那些乱石本可警告他不要抱希望——因为它们那副混沌的神情里，有某种东西与生养它们的这片原野同出一脉，它们隐隐暗示：这片原野是无穷无尽的。

## XVII The Unicorn Comes in the Starlight / 十七 独角兽于星光中降临

===Original===
And winter came, and whitened the roofs of Erl, and all the forest and uplands. And when Orion took his hounds afield in the morning the world lay like a book that was newly written by Life; for all the story of the night before lay in long lines in the snow. Here the fox had gone and there the badger, and here the red deer had gone out of the wood; the tracks led over the downs and disappeared from sight, as the deeds of statesmen, soldiers, courtiers and politicians appear and disappear on the pages of history. Even the birds had their record on those white downs, where the eye could follow each step of their treble claws, till suddenly on each side of the track would appear three little scars where the tips of their longest feathers had flicked the snow, and there the track faded utterly. They were like some popular cry, some vehement fancy, that comes down on a page of history for a day, and passes, leaving no other record at all except those lines on one page.

===Chinese===
于是冬日降临，将厄尔的屋宇、一切森林与高地尽皆染白。清晨奥利安带着他的群犬出猎时，整个世界宛如一部由生命新书写就的书卷；因为前夜的全部故事都化作长长的线条留在了雪上。狐狸从这儿走过，獾从那儿走过，赤鹿从这里出了林子；足迹越过丘陵，消失于视线之外，恰如政治家、军人、廷臣与政客的所作所为在历史的书页上出现又消逝。就连飞鸟也在那些白色的丘陵上留下了它们的记录，目光可追随它们三趾爪的每一步，直到足迹两侧忽然现出三道细小的擦痕——那是它们最长羽毛的梢头拂过积雪所留——足迹便在那里彻底隐没了。它们就像某种一时喧嚣的呼号，某种激烈的狂想，在历史的某一页上降临一日，便消逝无踪，除了那一页上的几道线条之外，不留任何别的痕迹。

===Original===
And amongst all these records left of the story of night Orion would choose the track of some great stag not too long gone, and would follow it with his hounds away over the downs until even the sound of his horn could be heard no longer in Erl. And over a ridge with his hounds, he and they all black against red remnants of sunset, the folk of Erl would see him coming home; and often it was not until all the stars were glowing through the frost. Often the skin of a red deer hung over his shoulders and the huge horns bobbed and nodded above his head.

===Chinese===
在夜之故事所留下的这一切记录之中，奥利安会挑出某只离去未久的大牡鹿的足迹，带着群犬循之越过丘陵远去，直追到他的号角声在厄尔也再听不见。厄尔之民会看见他带着群犬翻过一道山脊归家，他与群犬的剪影在落日殷红的余晖映衬之下通体漆黑；而往往已是满天星辰透过寒霜闪烁的时分。常有赤鹿的皮毛披在他肩上，巨大的鹿角在他头顶上下颠动。

===Original===
And at this time there met one day in the forge of Narl, all unknown to Orion, the men of the parliament of Erl. They met after sunset when all were home from their work. And gravely Narl handed to each the mead that was brewed from the clover honey; and when all were come they sat silent. And then Narl broke the silence, saying that Alveric ruled over Erl no more and his son was Lord of Erl, and telling again how once they had hoped for a magic lord to rule over the valley and to make it famous, and saying that this should be he. “And where now,” he said, “is the magic for which we hoped? For he hunts the deer as all his forefathers hunted, and nothing of magic has touched him from over there; and there is no new thing.”

===Chinese===
约莫在这时候，有一天，厄尔议会的众人在纳尔（Narl）的铁匠铺里聚首了，奥利安全然不知此事。他们在日落之后众人做完各自的活计回家时相聚。纳尔郑重地递给每人一杯以三叶草蜂蜜酿成的蜂蜜酒；人到齐之后，众人默然静坐。于是纳尔打破了沉默，说阿尔韦里克已不再治理厄尔，他的儿子作了厄尔领主，又重提他们曾经如何盼望着有一位通魔法的领主来治理这座山谷、使之名扬天下，并说此人当是他。「然而我们曾期盼的魔法，」他说，「如今何在？因为他打猎逐鹿，一如他列祖列宗之所为，那边没有任何魔法之物沾染过他；这里也没有任何新鲜事物。」

===Original===
And Oth stood up to defend him. “He is as fleet as his hounds,” he said, “and hunts from dawn to sunset, and crosses the furthest downs and comes home untired.”

“It is but youth,” said Guhic. And so said all but Threl.

And Threl stood up and said: “He has a knowledge of the ways of the woods, and the lore of the beasts, beyond the learning of man.”

“You taught him,” said Guhic. “There is no magic here.”

“Nothing of this,” said Narl, “is from over there.”

===Chinese===
于是奥斯（Oth）站起来为他辩护。「他迅捷如他的群犬，」他说，「从黎明猎到日落，越过最远的丘陵归来而不疲倦。」

「那不过是年轻罢了。」古希克（Guhic）说。除斯瑞尔（Threl）外众人皆附和。

斯瑞尔站起来说：「他通晓林间之道，熟谙百兽之识，非人力所学能及。」

「那是你教的。」古希克说，「这里没有什么魔法。」

「这些，」纳尔说，「没有一样是从那边来的。」

===Original===
Thus they argued awhile lamenting the loss of the magic for which they had hoped: for never a valley but history touches it once, never a village but once its name is awhile on the lips of men; only the village of Erl was utterly unrecorded; never a century knew it beyond the round of its downs. And now all their plans seemed lost which they made so long ago, and they saw no hope except in the mead that was brewed from the clover honey. To this they turned in silence. Now it was a goodly brew.

===Chinese===
他们如此争辩了一阵，哀叹他们所期盼的魔法已然落空：因为从来没有哪座山谷不被历史触碰过一次，从来没有哪个村子其名字不曾有一时停留在人们唇间；唯独厄尔这个村子全然不见于记载；没有一个世纪知道它，只知道它在自家丘陵的环绕之中。如今他们许久以前所订下的一切计划似乎尽成泡影，除了那以三叶草蜂蜜酿成的蜂蜜酒之外，他们看不到别的指望。他们便默然转向了酒。这确是一等佳酿。

===Original===
And in a while new plans flashed clear in their minds, new schemes, new devices; and debates in the parliament of Erl flowed proudly on. And they would have made a plan and a policy; but Oth arose from his seat. There was in a flint-built house in the village of Erl an ancient chronicle, a volume bound in leather, and in it at certain seasons folk wrote all manner of things, the wisdom of farmers concerning the time to sow, the wisdom of hunters concerning the tracking of stags, and the wisdom of prophets that told of the way of Earth. From this Oth quoted now, two lines that he remembered on one of the aged pages; and all the rest of that page told of hoeing; these lines he said to the parliament of Erl as they sat with the mead before them at their table:

> “Hooded, and veiled with their night-like tresses,
 The Fates shall bring what no prophet guesses.”

And then they planned no more, for either their minds were calmed by a certain awe that they seemed to find in the lines, or it may be the mead was stronger than anything written in books. However it be they sat silent over their mead. And in early starlight while the west still glowed they passed away from Narl’s house back to their own homes grumbling as they went that they had no magic lord to rule over Erl, and yearning for magic, to save from oblivion the village and valley they loved. They parted one by one as they came to their houses. And three or four that dwelt near the end of the village on the side that was under the downs were not yet come to their doors, when, white and clear in the starlight and what remained of the gloaming, they saw hard-pressed and wearied a hunted unicorn coming across the downs. They stopped and gazed and shaded their eyes and stroked their beards and wondered. And still it was a white unicorn galloping wearily. And then they heard drawing nearer the cry of Orion’s hounds.

===Chinese===
过了一会儿，新的计划在他们心中闪现得清晰起来，新的谋略、新的筹策；厄尔议会的辩论豪迈地流淌开去。他们本来就要订出一项计划、一条方略来了；可是奥斯从座上站了起来。在厄尔村中一座燧石造的屋子里，有一部古老的编年史，一卷皮面装订的书册，每逢特定的时节，人们便在其中写下各种各样的事情：农人关于播种时令的智慧，猎人关于追踪牡鹿的智慧，以及先知们讲述人间之道的智慧。奥斯此刻便从中引述了两行——他记得其中一页老旧书页上的句子；而那一页其余的内容讲的尽是锄地之事；他把这两行诵给厄尔议会众人听，其时众人正坐在桌前，面前摆着蜂蜜酒：

> 「兜帽掩覆，夜色般的长发如纱垂面，
 命运女神将携来先知所不能臆测之物。」

此后他们便不再谋划了，因为不知是他们在那两行诗中似乎寻获的某种敬畏使心安定下来，抑或是那蜂蜜酒比书上所写的任何东西都更为烈性。无论如何，他们默然坐着饮酒。在初升的星光下，西方天际犹有余晖，他们离开纳尔的屋子各自回家，一路抱怨着厄尔没有一位通魔法的领主来治理，渴盼着魔法降临，以拯救他们所爱的村与谷免于湮没无闻。他们走到各自家门，便一个接一个地散去。有三四个住在村子尽头、丘陵那一侧的人尚未走到自家门口，忽然在星光与残存的暮色中，洁白而清晰，他们看见一只被追猎的独角兽正疲态尽显、狼狈不堪地越过丘陵而来。他们停下脚步凝目望去，用手遮住眉眼，抚着胡须，暗暗称奇。那始终是一匹白色的独角兽，正疲惫地奔驰着。随后他们听见了奥利安群犬的吠声，渐次逼近。

## V The Wisdom of the Parliament of Erl / 五 厄尔议会的智慧

===Original===
In those bridal days the men of Erl came often to the castle, bringing gifts and felicitations; and in the evenings they would talk in their houses of the fair things that they hoped for the Vale of Erl on account of the wisdom of the thing they had done when they spoke with the old lord in his long red room.

There was Narl the blacksmith, who had been their leader; there was Guhic, who first had thought of it, after speaking with his wife, an upland farmer of clover pastures near Erl; there was Nehic a driver of horses; there were four vendors of beeves; and Oth, a hunter of deer; and Vlel the master-ploughman: all these and three men more had gone to the Lord of Erl and made that request that had set Alveric on his wanderings. And now they spoke of all the good that would come of it. They had all desired that the Vale of Erl should be known among men, as was, they felt, its desert. They had looked in histories, they had read books treating of pasture, yet seldom found mention at all of the vale they loved. And one day Guhic had said “Let all us people be ruled in the future by a magic lord, and he shall make the name of the valley famous, and there shall be none that have heard not the name of Erl.”

And all had rejoiced and had made a parliament; and it had gone, twelve men, to the Lord of Erl. And it had been as I have told.

So now they spoke over their mead of the future of Erl, and its place among other valleys, and of the reputation that it should have in the world. They would meet and talk in the great forge of Narl, and Narl would bring them mead from an inner room, and Threl would come in late from his work in the woods. The mead was of clover honey, heavy and sweet; and when they had sat awhile in the warm room, talking of daily things of the valley and uplands, they would turn their minds to the future, seeing as through a golden mist the glory of Erl. One praised the beeves, another the horses, another the good soil, and all looked to the time when other lands should know the great mastery among valleys that was held by the valley of Erl.

And Time that brought these evenings bore them away, moving over the Vale of Erl as over all fields we know, and it was spring again and the season of bluebells. And one day in the prime of the wild anemones, it was told that Alveric and Lirazel had a son.

===Chinese===
在那些新婚的日子里，厄尔的人常常来到城堡，带着礼物与道贺；到了傍晚，他们便在自己家中谈论他们所盼的种种美事——这些美事，他们以为都将降临于厄尔之谷，皆因他们当初在那间长长的红房间里与老领主进言时，做下了那桩明智之举。

那其中曾是他们首领的，是铁匠纳尔（Narl）；有古希克（Guhic），他原是厄尔附近一片苜蓿高坡上的农夫，是与妻子说过话之后最先想到此事的人；有尼希克（Nehic），是个赶马的人；还有四个卖牛肉的；有奥斯（Oth），是个猎鹿的；还有扶犁的好手弗莱尔（Vlel）：这些人，再加上另外三个，曾一同去见厄尔领主，提出了那个请求，遂使阿尔韦里克踏上了他的漂泊之旅。如今他们便谈论起由此将要生出的一切好处。他们都巴不得厄尔之谷能为人所知，一如他们所觉得它所应得的那般。他们翻过史书，读过专论牧场的书，却很少寻见他们所爱的这片山谷被提及半句。于是一天古希克说道：「将来让我们所有的人都受一位通魔法的领主来统治吧，他必使这山谷之名传扬四海，再无人不曾听见厄尔之名。」

众人都欢喜起来，便组成了一届议会；议会一行十二人，去了厄尔领主那里。其事即如我所述。

于是如今他们一边饮着蜂蜜酒，一边谈论厄尔的未来、它在众山谷之间所应有的位置、以及它在世上所应有的声誉。他们常在纳尔那宽大的铁匠铺里相聚谈论，纳尔会从内室取出蜂蜜酒来给他们，斯瑞尔（Threl）则从林中的活计里迟迟归来。那酒是苜蓿蜜所酿，醇厚而甘甜；当他们在那暖屋里坐上一会儿，谈着山谷与高地上的日常琐事，便将心思转向未来，如同隔着一层金色的薄雾望见厄尔的荣光。这一个称赞牛肉，那一个称赞马匹，又一个称赞那肥沃的土壤，众人皆盼着有朝一日他邦之土也能知晓厄尔之谷在众山谷之中所握有的那般无人能及的卓绝。

而带来这些黄昏的光阴，又将它们一一带走，时光这般掠过厄尔之谷，如同掠过我们所知的一切原野，于是又是春天，又是蓝风信花开的时节。一日，正值野白头翁花盛开之时，有人报说阿尔韦里克与莉拉泽尔生了一个儿子。

===Original===
Then all the people of Erl lit a fire next night on the hill, and danced about it and drank mead and rejoiced. All day they had dragged logs and branches for it from a wild wood near, and the glow of the fire was seen in other lands. Only on the pale-blue peaks of the mountains of Elfland no gleam of it shone, for they are unchanged by ought that can happen here.

And when they rested from dancing round their fires they would sit on the ground and foretell the fortune of Erl, when it should be ruled over by this son of Alveric with all the magic he would have from his mother. And some said he would lead them to war, and some said to deeper ploughing; and all foretold a better price for their beeves. None slept that night for dancing and foretelling a glorious future, and for rejoicing at the things they foretold. And above all they rejoiced that the name of Erl should be thenceforth known and honoured in other lands.

Then Alveric sought for a nurse for his child, all through the valley and uplands, and not easily found any worthy of having the care of one that was of the royal line of Elfland; and those that he found were frightened of the light, as though not of our earth or sky, that seemed to shine at times in the baby’s eyes. And in the end he went one windy morning up the hill of the lonely witch, and found her sitting idly in her doorway, having nothing to curse or bless.

===Chinese===
于是次日之夜，厄尔全境的百姓在山岗上点起了火，围着火跳舞、饮酒、欢腾。他们一整天都从附近的野林中拖来圆木与树枝以供此火，那火光连别处的地方都能望见。唯有在精灵国群山那淡蓝色的峰巅上，不见它的一丝微光，因为那些山峰不为人世所能发生的任何事所改变。

当他们围着火跳累了歇息时，便坐在地上预言厄尔的运数——等阿尔韦里克这个儿子将来从母亲那里承下全部魔法、统治此地之时。有人说他将领他们去打仗，有人说他将领他们更深地犁地；众人都预言他们的牛肉能卖个更好的价钱。那一夜无人入睡，全都跳舞、预言一个光辉的未来，又为他们所预言的种种而欢欣。而尤其令他们欢喜的是，厄尔之名从今以后将在他邦为人所知、为人所敬。

于是阿尔韦里克为他的孩子寻一位乳母，寻遍了山谷与高地，却不容易寻得一个配照管精灵国王室血脉的人；他所寻见的那些，又都被那偶尔似乎闪耀在婴孩眼中的光所惊吓——那光仿佛不属于我们的天地。末了，在一个刮风的早晨，他登上孤身女巫所居的山岗，见她正懒懒地坐在自家门口，既无事可咒，也无事可祝。

===Original===
“Well,” said the witch, “did the sword bring you fortune?”

“Who knows,” said Alveric, “what brings fortune, since we cannot see the end?”

And he spoke wearily, for he was weary with age, and never knew how many years had gone over him on the day he travelled to Elfland; far more it seemed than had passed on that same day over Erl.

“Aye,” said the witch. “Who knows the end but we?”

“Mother Witch,” said Alveric, “I wedded the King of Elfland’s daughter.”

“That was a great advancement,” said the old witch.

“Mother Witch,” said Alveric, “we have a child. And who shall care for him?”

“No human task,” said the witch.

“Mother Witch,” said Alveric, “will you come to the Vale of Erl and care for him and be the nurse at the castle? For none but you in all these fields knows ought of the things of Elfland, except the princess, and she knows nothing of Earth.”

And the old witch answered: “For the sake of the King I will come.”

So the witch came down from the hill with a bundle of queer belongings. And thus the child was nursed in the fields we know by one who knew songs and tales of his mother’s country.

===Chinese===
「呵，」女巫说，「那剑可曾给你带来好运？」

「谁知道，」阿尔韦里克说，「什么能带来好运呢，既然我们看不见结局？」

他说得疲倦，因为岁月已使他疲倦，他永远不知道在他奔赴精灵国的那一日，究竟有多少个年头从他身上走过；那似乎远远多过在同一日里走过厄尔的年头。

「是啊，」女巫说，「除了我们，又有谁知道结局呢？」

「女巫妈妈，」阿尔韦里克说，「我娶了精灵国王的女儿。」

「那可是一桩大造化，」老女巫说。

「女巫妈妈，」阿尔韦里克说，「我们有了一个孩子。可谁来照管他呢？」

「这不是人间的活计，」女巫说。

「女巫妈妈，」阿尔韦里克说，「你可愿到厄尔之谷来照管他，到城堡里做乳母？因为在这些原野上，除你之外再无人懂得精灵国之事——公主除外，可她对人间一无所知。」

老女巫答道：「为了精灵国王的缘故，我去。」

于是女巫带着一包稀奇古怪的物事下了山。这孩子便在我们所知的原野上，由一位熟知他母亲之国的歌谣与故事的人所哺养。

===Original===
And often, as they bent together over the baby, that aged witch and the Princess Lirazel would talk together, and afterwards through long evenings, of things about which Alveric knew nothing: and for all the age of the witch, and the wisdom that she had stored in her hundred years, which is all hidden from man, it was nevertheless she who learned when they talked together, and the Princess Lirazel who taught. But of Earth and the ways of Earth Lirazel never knew anything.

And this old witch that watched over the baby so tended him and so soothed, that in all his infancy he never wept. For she had a charm for brightening the morning, and a charm for cheering the day, and a charm for calming a cough, and a charm for making the nursery warm and pleasant and eerie, when the fire leaped up at the sound of it, from logs that she had enchanted, and sent large shadows of the things about the fire quivering dark and merry over the ceiling.

And the child was cared for by Lirazel and the witch as children are cared for whose mothers are merely human; but he knew tunes and runes besides, that other children hear not in fields we know.

So the old witch moved about the nursery with her black stick, guarding the child with her runes.
